  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kurcaci (dwarf) adalah makhluk gaib berwujud manusia dalam cerita rakyat Jermanik, yang umumnya tinggal di gunung atau batu dan dikenal sebagai perajin yang mahir.",
    "en": "A dwarf is a supernatural human-shaped being of Germanic folklore, commonly said to live in mountains or stones and known as a skilled craftsman.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "dwarf-folklore-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Kurcaci (dwarf) adalah makhluk gaib berwujud manusia dalam cerita rakyat Jermanik (Germanic). Gambaran tentang mereka sangat beragam sepanjang sejarah; umumnya, tetapi tidak selalu, mereka tinggal di gunung atau batu dan merupakan perajin yang mahir. Dalam sumber sastra awal hanya lelaki yang secara tegas disebut kurcaci, meski mereka disebut punya saudari dan anak perempuan. Kata Inggris dwarf berasal dari dweorg (Old English), sekerabat dengan dvergr (Old Norse) dan twerg (Old High German).",
      "en": "A dwarf is a supernatural human-shaped being of Germanic folklore. Accounts of dwarfs vary widely through history; they are commonly, though not always, said to live in mountains or stones and to be skilled craftsmen. In early literary sources only males are explicitly called dwarfs, though they are said to have sisters and daughters. The English word dwarf comes from Old English dweorg, related to Old Norse dvergr and Old High German twerg.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dwarf-folklore-c01",
        "dwarf-folklore-c02",
        "dwarf-folklore-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut Edda Prosa, para kurcaci mula-mula tumbuh seperti belatung dalam daging raksasa Ymer, lalu atas keputusan para dewa memperoleh akal dan rupa manusia, tetapi tetap harus tinggal di dalam tanah dan batu; Modsogner dan Durin adalah dua di antaranya. Syair Völuspá juga menyebut bahwa Motsognir adalah yang terkuat di antara para kurcaci, dan Durin berikutnya. Para sarjana menduga Svartálfar (\"elf hitam\") dalam Edda Prosa adalah makhluk yang sama dengan kurcaci, karena keduanya disebut tinggal di Svartálfheimr.",
      "en": "According to the Prose Edda, the dwarfs first quickened like maggots in the flesh of the giant Ymer, then by the gods' decision gained understanding and the likeness of men, yet still had to dwell in earth and rocks; Modsogner and Durin were two of them. The poem Völuspá likewise calls Motsognir the mightiest of the dwarfs, with Durin next. Scholars take the Svartálfar (\"black elves\") of the Prose Edda to be the same beings as dwarfs, since both are said to live in Svartálfheimr.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dwarf-folklore-c04",
        "dwarf-folklore-c11",
        "dwarf-folklore-c12",
        "dwarf-folklore-c15"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam sumber Edda, kurcaci membuat harta ajaib bagi para dewa, seperti Mjölnir, rambut Sif, Draupnir, Gullinbursti, Skíðblaðnir, Gleipnir, dan Gungnir, serta Brísingamen bagi Freyja; mereka juga membuat mead puisi dari darah Kvasir. Dalam kesusastraan Jerman banyak kurcaci dapat menjadi tak kasatmata, biasanya dengan Tarnkappe (jubah tak kasatmata), dan bisa bersikap bermusuhan atau bersahabat terhadap manusia. Kurcaci Alberich dalam Nibelungenlied menjaga harta Nibelung dan sekuat dua belas lelaki.",
      "en": "The Eddic dwarfs are the makers of the gods' wonders, among them Gungnir, Gleipnir, Draupnir, Mjölnir, Gullinbursti, Skíðblaðnir and the golden hair of Sif, as well as Freyja's Brísingamen; they also brewed the mead of poetry from Kvasir's blood. In German literature many dwarfs can become invisible, usually with a Tarnkappe (cloak of invisibility), and may be hostile or friendly to humans. The dwarf Alberich in the Nibelungenlied guards the Nibelung treasure and has the strength of twelve men.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dwarf-folklore-c05",
        "dwarf-folklore-c06",
        "dwarf-folklore-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam sumber Nordik awal kurcaci tidak disebut kecil, tetapi dalam saga legenda dan cerita rakyat kemudian mereka sering digambarkan pendek; sebagian dapat berubah wujud, seperti Andvari yang hidup sebagai ikan tombak. Dalam syair Alvissmol, kurcaci Alvis yang ingin menikahi putri Thor dipaksa menjawab banyak pertanyaan hingga fajar, lalu Thor berkata \"hari telah menangkapmu, kurcaci\" ketika matahari bersinar di aula. Menurut artikel id, kurcaci tinggal di gua di pegunungan atau di bawah tanah, dapat melihat dengan baik dalam gelap, menambang emas, perak, dan besi, dan merupakan pandai besi yang mahir; dalam mitologi Nordik mereka membuat rantai yang mampu mengikat serigala Fenrir.",
      "en": "In early Norse sources dwarfs are not said to be small, but in the legendary sagas and later folklore they are often described as short; some can change shape, like Andvari who lived as a pike. In the poem Alvissmol, the dwarf Alvis, wishing to marry Thor's daughter, is made to answer question after question until dawn, and Thor says \"the day has caught thee, dwarf\" as the sun shines in the hall. According to the id article, dwarfs live in caves in the mountains or underground, see well in the dark, mine gold, silver and iron, and are skilled smiths; in Norse mythology they made the chain able to bind the wolf Fenrir.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dwarf-folklore-c08",
        "dwarf-folklore-c09",
        "dwarf-folklore-c10",
        "dwarf-folklore-c13",
        "dwarf-folklore-c14",
        "dwarf-folklore-c16",
        "dwarf-folklore-c20"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kurcaci tetap muncul dalam cerita rakyat modern wilayah berbahasa Jermanik, misalnya Simonside Dwarfs di Northumberland yang konon memakai cahaya untuk menyesatkan orang seperti will-o'-the-wisp. Dalam budaya populer mereka tampil dalam karya J. R. R. Tolkien dan Terry Pratchett, dan dalam dongeng Putih Salju digambarkan sebagai bangsa pekerja keras yang polos tetapi melindungi.",
      "en": "Dwarfs remain in the modern folklore of Germanic-speaking regions, such as the Simonside Dwarfs of Northumberland, said to use lights to lure people off paths like a will-o'-the-wisp. In popular culture they appear in the works of J. R. R. Tolkien and Terry Pratchett, and in the tale of Snow White they are a hard-working, simple but protective people.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dwarf-folklore-c17",

        "dwarf-folklore-c19"
      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c01",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "is a type of supernatural human-shaped being in Germanic folklore. Accounts of dwarfs vary significantly throughout history. They are commonly, but not exclusively, presented as living in mountains or stones and being skilled craftsmen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurcaci adalah makhluk gaib berwujud manusia dalam cerita rakyat Jermanik (Germanic), umumnya tinggal di gunung atau batu dan perajin mahir.",
        "en": "A dwarf is a supernatural human-shaped being in Germanic folklore, commonly living in mountains or stones and a skilled craftsman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c02",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "In early literary sources, only males are explicitly referred to as dwarfs. However, they are described as having sisters and daughters, while male and female dwarfs feature in later saga literature and folklore.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sumber awal hanya lelaki yang disebut kurcaci, tetapi mereka punya saudari dan anak perempuan.",
        "en": "In early sources only males are called dwarfs, but they have sisters and daughters."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c03",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The modern English noun dwarf descends from Old English: dweorg . It has a variety of cognates in other Germanic languages, including Old Norse dvergr, Old Frisian dwerch, Middle Dutch dwerch, Middle Low German dwerch, and Old High German twerg.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata Inggris (English) dwarf berasal dari dweorg (Old English), sekerabat dengan dvergr (Old Norse) dan twerg (Old High German).",
        "en": "English dwarf descends from Old English dweorg, cognate with Old Norse dvergr and Old High German twerg."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c04",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s3",
      "quote": "The dwarfs had first been created and had quickened in Ymer’s flesh, and were then maggots; but now, by the decision of the gods, they got the understanding and likeness of men, but still had to dwell in the earth and in rocks. Modsogner was one dwarf and Durin another.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Edda Prosa (Anderson): kurcaci (dwarfs) mula-mula belatung (maggots) dalam daging Ymer; para dewa memberi akal dan rupa manusia, tetapi mereka tinggal di tanah dan batu (rocks); Modsogner dan Durin.",
        "en": "Prose Edda (Anderson): the dwarfs were first maggots in Ymer's flesh; the gods gave them understanding and human likeness, but they dwell in earth and rocks; Modsogner and Durin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c05",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "In Eddic sources dwarfs are attributed with creating magical treasures for the gods such as Mjölnir, Sif's hair, Draupnir, Gullinbursti, Skíðblaðnir, Gleipnir and Gungnir, while in Sörla þáttr they craft Brísingamen for Freyja. They further created the Mead of Poetry from the blood of Kvasir",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sumber Edda (Eddic), kurcaci membuat Mjölnir, rambut Sif, Draupnir, Gullinbursti, Skíðblaðnir, Gleipnir, Gungnir, Brísingamen bagi Freyja, dan mead puisi (Mead of Poetry) dari darah Kvasir.",
        "en": "In Eddic sources dwarfs craft Mjölnir, Sif's hair, Draupnir, Gullinbursti, Skíðblaðnir, Gleipnir, Gungnir, Brísingamen for Freyja, and the Mead of Poetry from Kvasir's blood."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c06",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The dwarf Alberich plays a vital role in the Nibelungenlied, where he guards the Nibelung's treasure and has the strength of twelve men.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurcaci Alberich dalam Nibelungenlied menjaga harta Nibelung dan sekuat dua belas lelaki.",
        "en": "The dwarf Alberich in the Nibelungenlied guards the Nibelung's treasure with the strength of twelve men."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c07",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "In German literature, many dwarfs can make themselves invisible, typically via a \"Tarnkappe\" (cloak of invisibility), which has been suggested to be an ancient attribute of dwarfs. Depending on the story, they may be hostile or friendly to humans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sastra Jerman (German), banyak kurcaci menjadi tak kasatmata dengan Tarnkappe dan bisa bermusuhan atau bersahabat dengan manusia.",
        "en": "In German literature many dwarfs turn invisible via a Tarnkappe and may be hostile or friendly to humans."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c08",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "In the early Old Norse sources, dwarfs are typically described vaguely, with no reference to them being particularly small; in the legendary sagas and later folklore, however, they are often described as short.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sumber Old Norse awal, kurcaci tidak disebut kecil; dalam saga legenda dan cerita rakyat kemudian mereka sering digambarkan pendek.",
        "en": "Early Old Norse sources do not call dwarfs small; legendary sagas and later folklore often describe them as short."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c09",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s5",
      "quote": "the dwarf Alvis, desirous of marrying Thor's daughter, is compelled by the god to answer a number of questions to test his knowledge.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Alvissmol (Bellows): kurcaci (dwarf) Alvis, yang ingin menikahi putri Thor, dipaksa menjawab banyak pertanyaan (questions).",
        "en": "Alvissmol (Bellows): the dwarf Alvis, wishing to marry Thor's daughter, is compelled to answer many questions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c10",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s5",
      "quote": "But with treacherous wiles must I now betray thee: The day has caught thee, dwarf! (Now the sun shines here in the hall.)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Alvissmol: Thor berkata hari (day) telah menangkap sang kurcaci (dwarf) ketika matahari (sun) bersinar di aula (hall).",
        "en": "Alvissmol: Thor says the day has caught the dwarf as the sun shines in the hall."

    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c11",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s4",
      "quote": "There was Motsognir the mightiest made Of all the dwarfs, and Durin next; Many a likeness of men they made, The dwarfs in the earth, as Durin said.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Völuspá (Voluspo, Bellows): Motsognir yang terkuat di antara kurcaci (dwarfs), Durin berikutnya; kurcaci di dalam bumi (earth) membuat banyak rupa manusia (men).",
        "en": "Völuspá (Voluspo, Bellows): Motsognir the mightiest of the dwarfs, Durin next; the dwarfs in the earth made many likenesses of men."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c12",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Scholars have noted that the Svartálfar ('black elves') appear to be the same beings as dwarfs, given that both are described in the Prose Edda as the residents of Svartálfheimr.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para sarjana menilai Svartálfar (elf hitam) sama dengan kurcaci, karena keduanya tinggal di Svartálfheimr menurut Prose Edda.",
        "en": "Scholars note the Svartálfar (black elves) seem the same as dwarfs, both living in Svartálfheimr in the Prose Edda."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c13",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Kurcaci (bahasa Inggris: dwarf ) adalah manusia kerdil dalam mitologi Nordik.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurcaci (dwarf) adalah manusia kerdil dalam mitologi Nordik.",
        "en": "Kurcaci (dwarf) are small people (manusia kerdil) in Norse (Nordik) mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c14",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Mereka hidup di gua-gua di daerah pegunungan atau di bawah tanah. Mereka mampu beradaptasi di lingkungan bawah tanah dan mampu melihat dengan baik di dalam kegelapan. Di sana mereka menggali barang-barang tambang seperti emas, perak, besi, dan sebagainya. Mereka juga merupakan pembuat senjata dan tukang besi yang mahir.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurcaci hidup di gua pegunungan atau bawah tanah, melihat baik dalam kegelapan, menambang emas, perak, besi, dan merupakan tukang besi yang mahir.",
        "en": "Dwarfs live in mountain caves or underground (gua, bawah tanah), see well in darkness (kegelapan), mine gold and silver (emas, perak), and are skilled smiths (tukang besi)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c15",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The Codex Regius version of Völuspá records that dwarfs were produced out of the earth, while in the Prose Edda, they form like maggots in the flesh of Ymir, which became the earth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Völuspá dalam Codex Regius mencatat kurcaci muncul dari tanah, sedangkan Prose Edda menyebut mereka terbentuk seperti belatung dalam daging Ymir.",
        "en": "The Codex Regius Völuspá has dwarfs come out of the earth; the Prose Edda has them form like maggots in Ymir's flesh."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c16",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Dalam mitologi Nordik, mereka menciptakan rantai pusaka yang mampu mengikat serigala ganas, Fenrir.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Nordik, kurcaci menciptakan rantai pusaka yang mengikat serigala Fenrir.",
        "en": "In Norse (Nordik) mythology the dwarfs made the magic chain (rantai pusaka) that bound the wolf (serigala) Fenrir."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c17",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Dwarfs feature in the modern folklore of Germanic-speaking regions of Europe, such as the Simonside Dwarfs in Northumberland, who are sometimes believed to use lights to lure people off paths, akin to a will-o'-the-wisp.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Simonside Dwarfs di Northumberland konon memakai cahaya untuk menyesatkan orang, seperti will-o'-the-wisp.",
        "en": "The Simonside Dwarfs of Northumberland are said to use lights to lure people off paths, like a will-o'-the-wisp."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c18",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Dwarfs continue to feature in modern popular culture, such as in the works of J. R. R. Tolkien and Terry Pratchett",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurcaci tampil dalam budaya populer, seperti karya J. R. R. Tolkien dan Terry Pratchett.",
        "en": "Dwarfs feature in popular culture such as the works of J. R. R. Tolkien and Terry Pratchett."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c19",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Dalam kisah dongeng Putih Salju, kurcaci digambarkan sebagai bangsa pekerja keras, polos, sedikit serakah, penggerutu, tetapi juga pelindung. Dalam kisah-kisah The Lord of the Rings, kurcaci adalah salah satu ras yang menghuni Dunia Tengah.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam dongeng Putih Salju kurcaci pekerja keras, polos, dan pelindung; dalam The Lord of the Rings mereka ras Dunia Tengah.",
        "en": "In Snow White (Putih Salju) dwarfs are hard-working, simple and protective (pelindung); in The Lord of the Rings a race of Middle-earth (Dunia Tengah)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dwarf-folklore-c20",
      "source_id": "dwarf-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Diversity in appearance is not only seen between dwarfs throughout time and region but also with individual dwarfs, who can be capable of changing their shape and size, such as in Reginsmál, in which the dwarf Andvari lived as a pike in the water due to curse from a Norn",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurcaci dapat berubah bentuk dan ukuran; dalam Reginsmál kurcaci Andvari hidup sebagai ikan tombak (pike) karena kutukan Norn.",
        "en": "Individual dwarfs can change shape and size; in Reginsmál the dwarf Andvari lived as a pike due to a Norn's curse."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Leprechaun adalah makhluk gaib bertubuh kecil dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia, oleh sebagian digolongkan sebagai peri penyendiri, yang digambarkan sebagai lelaki kecil berjanggut yang suka berbuat usil.",
    "en": "The leprechaun is a diminutive supernatural being of Irish folklore, classed by some as a solitary fairy, pictured as a little bearded man fond of mischief.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Leprechaun (bahasa Irlandia: leipreachán) adalah makhluk gaib bertubuh kecil dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia (Irish folklore), yang oleh sebagian orang digolongkan sebagai sejenis peri penyendiri. Ia biasanya digambarkan sebagai lelaki kecil berjanggut bermantel dan bertopi yang suka berbuat usil; belakangan ia digambarkan sebagai pembuat sepatu yang menyimpan sepanci emas di ujung pelangi. Kata leprechaun berasal dari bahasa Irlandia Kuno luchorpán atau lupracán.",
      "en": "The leprechaun (Irish: leipreachán) is a diminutive supernatural being of Irish folklore, classed by some as a type of solitary fairy. It is usually pictured as a small bearded man in a coat and hat who gets up to mischief; in later times it became a shoemaker with a hidden pot of gold at the end of the rainbow. The word comes from Old Irish luchorpán or lupracán.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "leprechaun-c01",
        "leprechaun-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Rujukan tertua tentang leprechaun terdapat dalam kisah abad pertengahan Echtra Fergus mac Léti: Fergus mac Léti, raja Ulster, tertidur di pantai dan terbangun ketika diseret ke laut oleh tiga lúchorpáin; ia menangkap mereka, dan mereka memberinya tiga permintaan agar dilepaskan. Dalam cerita rakyat, leprechaun adalah makhluk penyendiri yang pekerjaan utamanya membuat dan menambal sepatu dan yang gemar bercanda; manusia yang menangkapnya dapat menolak melepaskannya sampai diberi kekayaan gaib.",
      "en": "The earliest known reference to the leprechaun is in the medieval tale Echtra Fergus mac Léti: the Ulster king Fergus mac Léti dozes off on the shore and awakes to find three lúchorpáin hauling him into the waves; he captures them, and they grant him three wishes for their release. In folklore the leprechaun is a solitary creature whose main work is making and cobbling shoes and who loves practical jokes; a human who catches one may refuse to free it until given supernatural wealth.",

      "en": "William Butler Yeats classed the leprechaun as a \"solitary fairy\" and said its wealth came from treasure-crocks buried in wartime.       "statement": {
        "id": "Pada perayaan Hari Santo Patrick (Santo Patrick), leprechaun dikatakan datang ke rumah orang.",
        "en": "On Saint Patrick's Day (Santo Patrick), the leprechaun is said to visit people's homes."
      }

    {
      "id": "leprechaun-c12",
      "source_id": "leprechaun-s1",
      "quote": "It is stressed that the leprechaun, though some may call it fairy, is clearly to be distinguished from the Aos Sí (or the 'good people') of the fairy mounds (sidhe) and raths.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Leprechaun dibedakan dari Aos Sí penghuni gundukan peri (fairy mounds).",
        "en": "The leprechaun is distinguished from the Aos Sí of the fairy mounds."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Goblin adalah makhluk humanoid kecil, aneh, dan sering jahat yang menonjol dalam cerita rakyat Eropa, terkenal karena sifatnya yang usil atau iblis dan kemampuannya berubah wujud.",
    "en": "A goblin is a small, grotesque and often malevolent humanoid creature prominent in European folklore, known for its mischievous or demonic nature and ability to shapeshift.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "goblin-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Goblin adalah makhluk humanoid yang kecil, aneh rupanya, dan sering jahat, yang menonjol dalam cerita rakyat Eropa (European folklore). Ia dikenal usil atau bersifat iblis, bertubuh kecil (dalam sebagian tradisi sekitar 30 cm), berbulu atau berkulit seperti kulit samak, dan dapat berubah wujud. Goblin dipercaya tinggal di bawah tanah atau di rumah-rumah, tempat mereka melakukan apa saja dari lelucon hingga pembunuhan. Kata ini juga sering dipakai sebagai sebutan umum untuk semua makhluk peri kecil.",
      "en": "A goblin is a small, grotesque and often malevolent humanoid creature prominent in European folklore. It is known for a mischievous or demonic nature, small stature (about 30 cm in some traditions), a furry or leathery look and the power to change shape. Goblins are believed to live underground or in households, where they do anything from pranks to murder. The word is also often used as a blanket term for all small fay creatures.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "goblin-c01",
        "goblin-c02",
        "goblin-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kata goblin masuk ke bahasa Inggris pada awal abad ke-14 dari bahasa Prancis Kuno gobelin, yang pertama tercatat pada 1195 dalam kronik L'Estoire de la guerre sainte karya rahib Ambroise; bentuk Latin Abad Pertengahannya, gobelinus, muncul sekitar 1140 dalam Historia ecclesiastica karya Orderic Vitalis untuk iblis yang diusir dari sebuah gereja di Évreux. Para sarjana mengaitkannya dengan kata Yunani kóbalos (\"bajingan\" atau \"roh usil\") atau dengan kobold, roh rumah Jermanik. Kata Wales coblyn, sejenis knocker, berasal dari gobelin melalui kata Inggris goblin.",
      "en": "The word goblin entered English in the early 14th century from Old French gobelin, whose earliest known use, in 1195, is in Ambroise's chronicle L'Estoire de la guerre sainte; its Medieval Latin form gobelinus appears around 1140 in Orderic Vitalis's Historia ecclesiastica for a demon driven out of a church at Évreux. Scholars link it to Greek kóbalos (\"rogue\" or \"mischievous sprite\") or to the Germanic household spirit kobold. The Welsh coblyn, a kind of knocker, comes from gobelin through English goblin.",

      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Inggris dan Skotlandia, goblin sering muncul sebagai brownie, roh rumah yang baik hati yang mengerjakan tugas rumah pada malam hari asal diberi bubur atau susu; boggart dari Yorkshire adalah varian yang lebih jahat. Menurut artikel id, goblin digambarkan sebagai makhluk kecil hijau bertelinga runcing yang serakah dan cinta emas; dalam versi fantasi lain mereka makhluk buas yang tinggal di gua-gua gelap atau di bawah tanah.",
      "en": "In English and Scottish folklore goblins often appear as brownies, kindly house spirits doing chores at night if given porridge or milk; the boggarts of Yorkshire are a more malevolent variant. (Latin Abad Pertengahan, Pertengahannya)",
        "en": "Gobelin was first attested in 1195 in L'Estoire de la guerre sainte by the Norman monk Ambroise; the French form goes back to Medieval Latin gobelinus, around 1140 in Orderic Vitalis's Historia ecclesiastica, for a demon expelled from a church in Évreux."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c04",
      "source_id": "goblin-s1",
      "quote": "The Welsh coblyn, a type of knocker, derives from the Old French gobelin via the English goblin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata Wales (Welsh) coblyn, sejenis knocker, berasal dari gobelin (Old French) melalui goblin (English).",
        "en": "The Welsh coblyn, a knocker, derives from Old French gobelin via English goblin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c05",
      "source_id": "goblin-s1",
      "quote": "In English and Scottish folklore, goblins often appear as brownies, benevolent household spirits that perform domestic chores such as threshing grain, churning butter, or tending livestock during the night, provided they receive a small offering like porridge or milk left by the hearth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Inggris dan Skotlandia (English, Scottish), goblin muncul sebagai brownie, roh rumah yang mengerjakan tugas malam hari bila diberi bubur (porridge) atau susu (milk).",
        "en": "In English and Scottish folklore goblins appear as brownies, household spirits doing night chores for an offering of porridge or milk."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c06",
      "source_id": "goblin-s1",
      "quote": "In contrast, boggarts from Yorkshire traditions represent a more malevolent variant",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebaliknya, boggart dari Yorkshire adalah varian yang lebih jahat (malevolent).",
        "en": "In contrast, boggarts from Yorkshire are a more malevolent variant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c07",
      "source_id": "goblin-s1",
      "quote": "Similar creatures include brownies, dwarves, duendes, gnomes, imps, leprechauns, and kobolds, but it is also commonly used as a blanket term for all small, fay creatures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk serupa goblin: brownie, kurcaci, duende, gnome, imp, leprechaun, kobold; kata ini juga sebutan umum (blanket term) semua makhluk peri kecil.",
        "en": "Similar creatures include brownies, dwarves, duendes, gnomes, imps, leprechauns and kobolds; it is also a blanket term for all small fay creatures."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c08",
      "source_id": "goblin-s1",
      "quote": "The term \"goblin\" entered English in the early 14th century, derived from the Anglo-Norman French gobelin or Old French gobelin",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata goblin masuk ke bahasa Inggris (English) awal abad ke-14 dari gobelin bahasa Prancis Kuno (Old French).",
        "en": "The term goblin entered English in the early 14th century from Old French gobelin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c09",
      "source_id": "goblin-s2",
      "quote": "Dalam cerita rakyat Eropa, Goblin merupakan bangsa peri atau makhluk supranatural. Ukuran tubuh mereka kecil dan kadang kala tidak terlihat oleh mata manusia. Mereka dilukiskan sebagai makhluk mungil berwarna hijau dan bertelinga runcing.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Eropa, Goblin bangsa peri, kecil, kadang tak terlihat, mungil berwarna hijau dan bertelinga runcing.",
        "en": "In European (Eropa) folklore Goblins are fairy folk (bangsa peri), small, sometimes unseen, little green beings with pointed ears (telinga runcing)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c10",
      "source_id": "goblin-s2",
      "quote": "Mereka juga sangat serakah dan cinta emas atau jenis perhiasan. Goblin juga erat kaitannya dengan api dan memiliki kemahiran untuk menciptakannya. Menurut versi lain dalam beberapa dongeng dan kisah fantasi, Goblin merupakan bangsa makhluk yang buas dan ganas. Mereka tinggal di gua-gua yang gelap atau di bawah tanah.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Goblin serakah dan cinta emas, terkait api (api), dan menurut versi lain buas serta tinggal di gua-gua gelap atau bawah tanah.",
        "en": "Goblins are greedy and love gold (emas), are tied to fire (api), and in other versions are savage (buas), living in dark caves (gua-gua) or underground."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c11",
      "source_id": "goblin-s1",
      "quote": "Scholars propose possible etymological connections to earlier languages, including Ancient Greek kóbalos, meaning \"rogue\" or \"mischievous sprite\", which may have influenced post-classical Latin forms like cobalus denoting a demon. Additionally, links have been suggested to Germanic kobold, a household spirit",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para sarjana mengaitkan goblin dengan kóbalos (Yunani Kuno, Ancient Greek, \"rogue\") dan dengan kobold, roh rumah Jermanik (Germanic).",
        "en": "Scholars link it to Ancient Greek kóbalos (\"rogue\") and to the Germanic household spirit kobold."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "goblin-c12",
      "source_id": "goblin-s2",
      "quote": "Dalam beberapa kasus, Goblin telah diklasifikasikan sebagai makhluk kecil yang terus-menerus mengganggu dan memiliki berbagai kemampuan magis.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Goblin kadang diklasifikasikan sebagai makhluk kecil pengganggu dengan berbagai kemampuan magis (kemampuan magis).",
        "en": "Goblins are sometimes classed as small pestering beings with various magical powers (kemampuan magis)."
      }
    }

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Vamana (Sanskerta वामन, \"kerdil\"), juga dikenal sebagai Trivikrama (\"tiga langkah\"), Urukrama, dan Upendra, adalah awatara dewa Hindu Wisnu (Vishnu). Ia awatara kelima Wisnu dan Dashavatara pertama pada Treta Yuga, sesudah Narasimha. Pertama disebut dalam Weda, Vamana paling sering dikaitkan dalam epos dan Purana Hindu dengan kisah merebut kembali tiga dunia (Trailokya) dari raja daitya Mahabali dengan tiga langkah, untuk memulihkan tatanan kosmos dan mendorong Mahabali ke dunia bawah. Ia yang termuda di antara para aditya, putra Aditi dan resi Kashyapa.",
      "en": "Vamana (Sanskrit वामन, \"dwarf\"), also called Trivikrama (\"three steps\"), Urukrama and Upendra, is an avatar of the Hindu god Vishnu. Coming after Narasimha, he is the fifth of Vishnu's avatars and the first Dashavatara of the Treta Yuga. Already named in the Vedas, Vamana is best known in the Hindu epics and Puranas to the tale of reclaiming the three worlds (Trailokya) from the daitya king Mahabali with three steps, restoring the cosmic order and pushing Mahabali into the netherworld. He is the youngest of the adityas, sons of Aditi and the sage Kashyapa.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "vamana-c01",
        "vamana-c02",
        "vamana-c03",
        "vamana-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel id, Wamana dilukiskan sebagai brahmana berraga anak kecil yang membawa kamandalu dan payung. Kisahnya dimuat dalam Bhagawatapurana, Brahmapurana, Padmapurana, Wamanapurana, dan Mahapurana lain: Raja Bali mengundang semua brahmana untuk diberi hadiah, dan meski dinasihati Sukracarya, ia mengabulkan permintaan brahmana kecil itu atas tanah seluas tiga langkah. Brahmana itu lalu membesar; langkah pertamanya menginjak swarga, langkah kedua bumi, dan untuk langkah ketiga Bali menyerahkan kepalanya. Terkesan oleh kedermawanannya, Wamana memberinya gelar Mahabali dan berjanji ia kelak menjadi Indra pada Manwantara berikutnya.",
      "en": "According to the id article, Wamana is pictured as a brahmin in a child's body carrying a kamandalu and an umbrella. His story is in the Bhagawatapurana, Brahmapurana, Padmapurana, Wamanapurana and other Mahapuranas: King Bali invited all brahmins to receive gifts, and despite Sukracarya's warning he granted the little brahmin's request for land three strides wide. The brahmin then grew; his first stride covered heaven (swarga), the second the earth, and for the third Bali offered his head. Impressed by his generosity, Wamana gave him the title Mahabali and promised he would become Indra in the next Manwantara.",

    },
    {
      "id": "Kisah Wamana dan Raja Bali diperingati setiap tahun dalam perayaan Onam di Kerala, India, tempat juga terdapat kuil khusus untuknya; kuil Wamana lain antara lain ada di Kanchipuram, dekat kuil Kamakshi.",
      "en": "The story of Wamana and King Bali is commemorated every year at the Onam festival in Kerala, India, where there is also a temple devoted to him; other Wamana temples include one in Kanchipuram near the Kamakshi temple.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Morgan le Fay adalah penyihir perempuan yang kuat dan ambigu dalam legenda King Arthur, kadang peri dan kadang manusia, kadang penolong dan kadang musuh Arthur.",
    "en": "Morgan le Fay is a powerful and ambiguous enchantress of the legend of King Arthur, sometimes a fairy and sometimes human, sometimes Arthur's helper and sometimes his foe.",

      "morgan-le-fay-c01"

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Morgan le Fay (bahasa Wales dan Kornwall: Morgen; bahasa Prancis modern: Fée Morgane) adalah penyihir perempuan yang kuat dan ambigu dalam legenda Raja Arthur (King Arthur), dan paling sering menjadi saudarinya. Menurut berbagai pengarang, ia peri atau manusia, baik atau jahat, saudari, saudari tiri, atau sama sekali tak berkerabat dengan Arthur. Sifatnya yang mendua, bisa baik dan bisa jahat, adalah ciri penting dalam banyak versi abad pertengahan dan sesudahnya, dan tokohnya mungkin berasal dari mitologi Wales.",
      "en": "Morgan le Fay (Welsh and Cornish: Morgen; Modern French: Fée Morgane) is a powerful and ambiguous enchantress of the legend of King Arthur, most often his sister. Depending on the author she is a fairy or a human, good or harmful, Arthur's sister, half-sister or no kin at all. Her unpredictable double nature, capable of good and evil, is central to many medieval and later versions, and her figure may have arisen from Welsh mythology.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "morgan-le-fay-c01",
        "morgan-le-fay-c02",
        "morgan-le-fay-c08",
        "morgan-le-fay-c13"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kisah tertua yang terdokumentasi, Vita Merlini karya Geoffrey of Monmouth (sekitar 1150), mengaitkan Morgan dengan Isle of Apples (Avalon), tempat Arthur dibawa setelah terluka parah dalam pertempuran Camlann, sebagai pemimpin sembilan saudari sakti; di sana dan dalam roman awal Chrétien de Troyes peran utamanya adalah penyembuh agung. Geoffrey juga menggambarkannya dapat berubah wujud. Dalam siklus prosa abad ke-13 dan Le Morte d'Arthur, ia putri bungsu Igraine dan suami pertamanya, sehingga Arthur adalah saudara tirinya; ia menikah dengan Urien, menjadi murid Merlin, dan memusuhi para kesatria Meja Bundar serta Guinevere.",
      "en": "The earliest documented account, Geoffrey of Monmouth's Vita Merlini (c. 1150), ties Morgan to the Isle of Apples (Avalon), where Arthur was carried after being mortally wounded at the Battle of Camlann, as leader of nine magical sisters; there and in the early romances of Chrétien de Troyes her chief role is that of a great healer. Geoffrey also shows her able to change shape. In the 13th-century prose cycles and Le Morte d'Arthur she is the youngest daughter of Igraine and her first husband, making Arthur her half-brother; she marries Urien, becomes Merlin's apprentice, and opposes knights of the Round Table and Guinevere.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "morgan-le-fay-c10",
        "morgan-le-fay-c12",
        "morgan-le-fay-c14"

      "id": "Dalam sebagian versi, termasuk karya Malory, Morgan adalah musuh terbesar Arthur, tetapi akhirnya ia berdamai dan tetap berperan membawa Arthur dalam perjalanan terakhirnya ke Avalon. Kisah-kisah abad pertengahan dan Renaisans sesudah Camlann menampilkannya sebagai ratu Avalon yang abadi. Dalam versi modern tokohnya sering dirancukan dengan saudarinya, Ratu Orkney, sehingga ia dijadikan ibu Mordred.",
      "en": "In some versions, including Malory's, Morgan is Arthur's greatest enemy, yet she finally reconciles with him and keeps her role of bearing him on his last journey to Avalon. Medieval and Renaissance tales set after Camlann show her as the immortal queen of Avalon. In modern versions her character is often merged with her sister the Queen of Orkney, which makes her Mordred's mother.",

    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",

    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-s2",
      "url": "https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morgan_le_Fay",
      "title": "Morgan le Fay",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (de)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "de",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c01",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "is a powerful and ambiguous enchantress from the legend of King Arthur, in which most often she and he are siblings. According to the different authors, she is a fairy or a human; beneficial or harmful; either a sister, half-sister, or unrelated to Arthur.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Morgan le Fay adalah penyihir perempuan yang kuat dan ambigu dalam legenda King Arthur, peri atau manusia, baik atau jahat, saudari atau tak berkerabat dengan Arthur.",
        "en": "Morgan le Fay is a powerful, ambiguous enchantress of the King Arthur legend, a fairy or human, good or harmful, Arthur's sister or no kin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c02",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "Her character may have originated from Welsh mythology as well as from other ancient and medieval myths and historical figures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokohnya mungkin berasal dari mitologi Wales (Welsh) serta mitos kuno dan abad pertengahan lainnya.",
        "en": "Her character may originate from Welsh mythology and other ancient and medieval myths."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c03",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "The earliest documented account, by Geoffrey of Monmouth in Vita Merlini (written c. 1150) refers to Morgan in association with the Isle of Apples (Avalon), to which Arthur was carried after having been fatally wounded at the Battle of Camlann, as the leader of the nine magical sisters unrelated to Arthur.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Vita Merlini karya Geoffrey of Monmouth (sekitar 1150) mengaitkan Morgan dengan Isle of Apples (Avalon), tempat Arthur dibawa setelah Battle of Camlann, sebagai pemimpin sembilan saudari sakti.",
        "en": "Geoffrey of Monmouth's Vita Merlini (c. 1150) links Morgan to the Isle of Apples (Avalon), where Arthur was carried after the Battle of Camlann, as leader of nine magical sisters."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c04",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "Romance authors of the late 12th century established Morgan as Arthur's supernatural elder sister.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pengarang roman akhir abad ke-12 menjadikan Morgan kakak perempuan Arthur yang gaib.",
        "en": "Late 12th-century romance authors made Morgan Arthur's supernatural elder sister."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c05",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "In the 13th-century prose cycles – and the later works based on them, including the influential Le Morte d'Arthur – she is usually described as the youngest daughter of Arthur's mother Igraine and her first husband. Arthur, son of Igraine and Uther Pendragon, is thus Morgan's half-brother",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam siklus prosa abad ke-13 dan Le Morte d'Arthur ia putri bungsu Igraine; Arthur, putra Igraine dan Uther Pendragon, adalah saudara tirinya.",
        "en": "In the 13th-century prose cycles and Le Morte d'Arthur she is Igraine's youngest daughter; Arthur, son of Igraine and Uther Pendragon, is her half-brother."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c06",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "In some variants, including in the popular retelling by Malory, Morgan is the greatest enemy of Arthur, scheming to usurp his throne and indirectly becoming an instrument of his death. However, she eventually reconciles with Arthur, retaining her original role of taking him on his final journey to Avalon.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian versi, termasuk karya Malory, Morgan musuh terbesar Arthur, tetapi akhirnya berdamai dan membawanya ke Avalon.",
        "en": "In some variants, including Malory's, Morgan is Arthur's greatest enemy, yet she reconciles and takes him to Avalon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c07",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "Medieval and Renaissance tales feature continuations from the aftermath of Camlann where Morgan appears as the immortal queen of Avalon",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah abad pertengahan dan Renaisans (Renaissance) sesudah Camlann menampilkan Morgan sebagai ratu Avalon yang abadi.",
        "en": "Medieval and Renaissance tales after Camlann show Morgan as the immortal queen of Avalon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c08",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "Morgan le Fay (/ˈmɔːr.ɡən lə ˈfeɪ/; Welsh and Cornish: Morgen; Modern French: Fée Morgane)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Morgan le Fay dalam bahasa Wales dan Kornwall (Welsh, Cornish) adalah Morgen, dalam bahasa Prancis modern (Modern French) Fée Morgane.",
        "en": "Morgan le Fay is Morgen in Welsh and Cornish and Fée Morgane in Modern French."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c09",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s2",
      "quote": "Morgan le Fay, auch bekannt als Morgaine, Morgain (la Fée) oder Morgana und unter weiteren Namen, zum Beispiel im Mittelhochdeutschen: Feimorgan, Feimurgân oder Famurgan/Fâmurgân, ist eine wichtige weibliche Figur in der Mythologie um König Artus, der Artussage.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Morgan le Fay, juga Morgana atau Feimorgan (Mittelhochdeutschen), adalah tokoh perempuan penting (weibliche Figur) dalam mitologi Raja Arthur (König Artus).",
        "en": "Morgan le Fay, also Morgana or Feimorgan (Mittelhochdeutschen), is an important female figure (weibliche Figur) of the King Arthur (König Artus) legend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c10",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s2",
      "quote": "Von Geoffrey von Monmouth wird sie sowohl als Heilerin dargestellt wie auch als jemand, der sich in unterschiedliche Gestalten verwandeln konnte.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Geoffrey (Geoffrey von Monmouth) menggambarkannya sebagai penyembuh (Heilerin) dan yang dapat berubah wujud (Gestalten verwandeln).",
        "en": "Geoffrey of Monmouth (Geoffrey von Monmouth) portrays her as a healer (Heilerin) who could change shape (Gestalten verwandeln)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c11",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "Notably, her modern character is frequently being conflated with that of her sister, the Queen of Orkney, thus making Morgan the mother of Arthur's son and nemesis Mordred.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh modernnya sering dirancukan dengan saudarinya, Queen of Orkney (Ratu Orkney), sehingga Morgan menjadi ibu Mordred.",
        "en": "Her modern character is often conflated with her sister the Queen of Orkney, making Morgan the mother of Mordred."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c12",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "The young Morgan unhappily marries Urien, with whom she has a son, Yvain. She becomes an apprentice of Merlin, and a capricious and vindictive adversary of some knights of the Round Table, all the while harbouring a special hatred for Arthur's wife Guinevere.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Morgan menikah dengan Urien, beranak Yvain, menjadi murid Merlin, memusuhi kesatria Round Table (Meja Bundar), dan membenci Guinevere.",
        "en": "Morgan marries Urien, has a son Yvain, becomes Merlin's apprentice, opposes knights of the Round Table and hates Guinevere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c13",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "A significant aspect in many of Morgan's medieval and later iterations is the unpredictable duality of her nature, with potential for both good and evil.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri penting banyak versi Morgan adalah sifat mendua (duality) yang tak terduga, bisa baik (good) dan jahat (evil).",
        "en": "A key aspect of many versions of Morgan is the unpredictable duality of her nature, capable of good and evil."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morgan-le-fay-c14",
      "source_id": "morgan-le-fay-s1",
      "quote": "Therein, and in the early chivalric romances by Chrétien de Troyes and others, Morgan's chief role is that of a great healer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Vita Merlini dan roman awal Chrétien de Troyes, peran utama Morgan adalah penyembuh agung (great healer).",
        "en": "In Vita Merlini and Chrétien de Troyes's early romances Morgan's chief role is a great healer."
      }
    }
  ],

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Epona adalah dewi kuda dan kuda poni dalam agama Galia-Romawi, juga dewi kesuburan, satu-satunya dewa Kelt yang akhirnya dipuja di kota Roma.",
    "en": "Epona is the goddess of horses and ponies in Gallo-Roman religion, also a fertility goddess, and the only Celtic deity ultimately worshipped in Rome itself.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "epona-c01",
      "epona-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam agama Galia-Romawi (Gallo-Roman), Epona adalah dewi kuda dan kuda poni, sekaligus dewi kesuburan dan dewi ibu. Ia sering digambarkan menunggang kuda dengan duduk menyamping, atau duduk di singgasana bersama anak-anak kuda, sambil membawa patera, tanduk kelimpahan, buah, atau roti. Ia mungkin juga memiliki aspek dewi kematian, dan diduga ia beserta kudanya mengantar jiwa ke alam baka. Para sarjana membandingkannya dengan tokoh legenda Wales Rhiannon, yang juga erat dengan kuda.",
      "en": "Gallo-Roman religion honoured Epona as protector of horses and ponies, and also as a mother goddess of fertility. She is often shown riding sidesaddle or enthroned with foals, holding a patera, cornucopia, fruit or bread. She may also have had the aspect of a death goddess, and she and her horses are thought to have escorted souls to the afterlife. Scholars compare her with the Welsh legendary figure Rhiannon, also closely tied to horses.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "epona-c01",
        "epona-c02",
        "epona-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Nama Epona (\"Great Mare\", kuda betina agung) berasal dari bahasa Galia (Gaulish), dari epos (\"kuda\"), yang berakar pada kata Proto-Indo-Eropa untuk kuda. Pemujaannya sebagai pelindung kavaleri tersebar luas di Kekaisaran Romawi antara abad ke-1 dan ke-3 M. Menurut artikel de, pemujaannya dibuktikan oleh sekitar 60 prasasti persembahan di seluruh wilayah Kelt; pasukan Romawi menjadikannya dewi kuda dan pasukan berkuda, dan pada akhir zaman kuno pestanya dirayakan di sekitar Mantua pada 18 Desember. Juvenal dan Apuleius menyebut patung-patungnya dipuja di kuil kecil di dekat kandang kuda.",
      "en": "The name Epona (\"Great Mare\") comes from Gaulish epos (\"horse\"), rooted in the Proto-Indo-European word for horse. Her worship as patroness of cavalry spread widely across the Roman Empire between the 1st and 3rd centuries AD. According to the de article her cult is attested by some 60 dedicatory inscriptions across the Celtic world; Roman troops adopted her as goddess of horses and cavalry, and in late antiquity her festival was held around Mantua on 18 December. Juvenal and Apuleius mention her images being worshipped in small shrines by stables.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "epona-c04",
        "epona-c05",
        "epona-c07",
        "epona-c08",
        "epona-c09"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "statement": {
        "id": "Para sarjana membandingkan Epona dengan tokoh legenda Wales (Welsh) Rhiannon yang juga terkait kuda.",
        "en": "Scholars compare Epona to the Welsh legendary figure Rhiannon, also tied to horses."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "epona-c04",
      "source_id": "epona-s1",
      "quote": "Epona's worship as the patroness of cavalry was widespread in the Roman Empire between the 1st and 3rd centuries AD. While adopted Celtic deities were usually only associated with specific localities, Epona was \"the sole Celtic divinity ultimately worshipped in Rome itself.\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemujaan Epona sebagai pelindung kavaleri tersebar di Kekaisaran Romawi (Roman Empire) abad ke-1 hingga ke-3; ia satu-satunya dewa Kelt (Celtic) yang dipuja di Roma (Rome).",
        "en": "Epona's worship as cavalry patroness spread through the Roman Empire in the 1st–3rd centuries; she was the sole Celtic deity worshipped in Rome."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "epona-c05",
      "source_id": "epona-s1",
      "quote": "The name Epona ('Great Mare') originates from Gaulish, an extinct Celtic language. It is derived from the reconstructed Proto-Indo-European word for horse: *éḱwos, which appeared in the reconstructed Proto-Celtic language as *ekʷos ('horse'), and later became the Celtic Gaulish epos ('horse').",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Epona (Great Mare) berasal dari bahasa Galia (Gaulish), dari epos (kuda), berakar pada kata Proto-Indo-Eropa (Proto-Indo-European) untuk kuda.",
        "en": "The name Epona (Great Mare) comes from Gaulish epos (horse), from the Proto-Indo-European word for horse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "epona-c06",
      "source_id": "epona-s2",
      "quote": "Epona, seltener auch Epana genannt, ist eine keltische Göttin der Fruchtbarkeit sowie die römische Göttin der Pferde.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Epona (juga Epana) adalah dewi kesuburan Kelt (keltische Göttin der Fruchtbarkeit) dan dewi kuda Romawi (römische Göttin der Pferde).",
        "en": "Epona (also Epana) is a Celtic fertility goddess (keltische Göttin der Fruchtbarkeit) and the Roman goddess of horses (Göttin der Pferde)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "epona-c07",
      "source_id": "epona-s2",
      "quote": "Die römischen Truppen übernahmen Epona als Göttin der Pferde und der Reiterei, wobei sie nicht nur als Schutzgöttin der Kavallerie, sondern auch der Wagenführer verehrt wurde. In der Spätantike wurde das Fest der Epona in der Umgebung von Mantua am 18. Dezember begangen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pasukan Romawi (römischen Truppen) menjadikan Epona dewi kuda dan pasukan berkuda (Kavallerie); pada akhir zaman kuno (Spätantike) pestanya di Mantua pada 18 Desember (Dezember).",
        "en": "Roman troops (römischen Truppen) adopted Epona as goddess of horses and cavalry (Kavallerie); in late antiquity (Spätantike) her feast was held near Mantua on 18 December (Dezember)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "epona-c08",
      "source_id": "epona-s2",
      "quote": "Nach Angaben bei Juvenal (Satiren 8,155–157) und Apuleius (Metamorphosen 3,27,2) wurden Figuren der Göttin in kleinen Schreinen bei Pferdeställen verehrt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Juvenal dan Apuleius, patung dewi (Figuren der Göttin) dipuja di kuil kecil (Schreinen) dekat kandang kuda (Pferdeställen).",
        "en": "According to Juvenal and Apuleius, images of the goddess (Figuren der Göttin) were worshipped in small shrines (Schreinen) by stables (Pferdeställen)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "epona-c09",
      "source_id": "epona-s2",
      "quote": "Die Verehrung Eponas war zu Zeiten der Kelten und Gallo-Römer in der Antike bis Spätantike im gesamten keltischen Raum (Celticum) verbreitet, was durch rund 60 Weiheinschriften bezeugt ist.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemujaan Epona tersebar di seluruh wilayah Kelt (Celticum), dibuktikan sekitar 60 prasasti persembahan (Weiheinschriften).",
        "en": "Epona's worship spread across the Celtic world (Celticum), attested by about 60 dedicatory inscriptions (Weiheinschriften)."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cernunnos adalah dewa Kelt bertanduk rusa yang namanya hanya jelas tercatat sekali, pada Pillar of the Boatmen dari Paris abad ke-1 M; ia dikaitkan dengan kesuburan, hewan liar, dan dunia bawah.",
    "en": "Cernunnos is an antlered Celtic god known by name from a single clear inscription, on the Pillar of the Boatmen made in Paris in the 1st century CE; he is linked with fertility, wild beasts and the underworld.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cernunnos-c01",
      "cernunnos-c02",
      "cernunnos-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cernunnos adalah dewa Kelt (Celtic) yang namanya hanya jelas tercatat sekali, pada Pillar of the Boatmen dari Paris abad ke-1 M, yang dikaitkan dengan gambar sosok tua bertanduk rusa dengan torc tergantung di tanduknya. Dari artefak itu, nama Cernunnos dipakai untuk sekelompok gambar dewa bertanduk rusa, sering tua dan duduk bersila, yang terkait dengan torc, ular bertanduk domba, lambang kesuburan, dan hewan liar, terutama rusa. Sebanyak 25 gambar tipe Cernunnos telah dikenali, terutama di Galia timur laut, tetapi juga sampai ke Italia (Val Camonica) dan Denmark (Gundestrup).",
      "en": "Cernunnos is a Celtic god known by name from one clear inscription only: the Pillar of the Boatmen, set up in Paris in the 1st century CE, where the name stands beside an image of an aged, antlered figure with torcs hanging from his horns. From this artefact the name has been applied to a cluster of images of an antlered god, often aged and cross-legged, linked with torcs, ram-horned serpents, fertility symbols and wild beasts, especially deer. As many as 25 Cernunnos-type images are known, mostly in north-eastern Gaul but as far as Italy (Val Camonica) and Denmark (Gundestrup).",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cernunnos-c01",
        "cernunnos-c02",
        "cernunnos-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Cernunnos ditafsirkan sebagai dewa kesuburan, dunia bawah, dan dwiarah. Pemujaannya, yang terbukti secara ikonografis sejak abad ke-4 SM, tampaknya tak banyak terpengaruh penaklukan Romawi atas Galia. Pillar of the Boatmen ditemukan pada 1711 di bawah paduan suara Notre-Dame de Paris; kini huruf-hurufnya rusak sehingga sebagian ahli membacanya Cernennos. Menurut artikel id, Cernunnos adalah dewa bertanduk penguasa tempat-tempat liar dan dewa tumbuh-tumbuhan, dijuluki \"Lord of the wild things\", dan pada abad pertengahan dipakai gereja Kristen sebagai lambang antikristus.",
      "en": "Interpreters have seen in Cernunnos a fertility god, an underworld god, or a god facing two ways. His cult, attested in images from as early as the 4th century BCE, seems largely untouched by the Roman conquest of Gaul. The Pillar of the Boatmen was found in 1711 under the choir of Notre-Dame de Paris; its lettering is now damaged, and some scholars read Cernennos. According to the id article, Cernunnos is a horned god ruling wild places and a god of vegetation, called the \"Lord of the wild things\", and in the Middle Ages the Christian church used him as a symbol of the antichrist.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cernunnos-c04",
        "cernunnos-c05",
        "cernunnos-c06",
        "cernunnos-c07",
        "cernunnos-c08",
        "cernunnos-c09"
      ]
    }

      "quote": "Cernunnos is a Celtic god whose name is only clearly attested once, on the 1st-century CE Pillar of the Boatmen from Paris, where it is identified with an image of an aged, antlered figure with torcs around his horns.",

    {

      "source_id": "cernunnos-s1",
      "quote": "the name \"Cernunnos\" has been applied to the members of an iconographic cluster, consisting of depictions of an antlered god (often aged and with crossed legs) associated with torcs, ram-horned (or ram-headed) serpents, symbols of fertility, and wild beasts (especially deer).",
      "locator": null,

    {
      "id": "cernunnos-c03",
      "source_id": "cernunnos-s1",
      "quote": "As many as 25 depictions of the Cernunnos-type have been identified. Though this iconographic group is best attested in north-eastern Gaul, depictions of the god have been identified as far off as Italy (Val Camonica) and Denmark (Gundestrup).",

      "source_id": "cernunnos-s1",
      "quote": "Cernunnos has been variously interpreted as a god of fertility, of the underworld, and of bi-directionality. His cult, attested iconographically as early as the 4th century BCE, seems to have been largely unaffected by the Roman conquest of Gaul",

        "en": "Cernunnos is read as a god of fertility, the underworld and bi-directionality; his cult from the 4th century BCE was largely unaffected by the Roman conquest of Gaul."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lugh adalah tokoh mitologi Irlandia dari Tuatha Dé Danann, digambarkan sebagai pejuang, raja, perajin ulung, dan penyelamat, yang dikaitkan dengan perayaan panen Lughnasadh.",
    "en": "Lugh is a figure of Irish mythology from the Tuatha Dé Danann, portrayed as warrior, king, master craftsman and saviour, and linked with the harvest festival of Lughnasadh.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lugh-c01",
      "lugh-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lugh atau Lug (bahasa Irlandia modern: Lú) adalah tokoh dalam mitologi Irlandia (Irish mythology). Sebagai anggota Tuatha Dé Danann, sekelompok makhluk gaib, ia digambarkan sebagai pejuang, raja, perajin ulung, dan penyelamat, yang menguasai banyak keahlian termasuk seni. Ia juga dikaitkan dengan sumpah, kebenaran, dan hukum, sehingga dengan kerajaan yang sah. Julukannya yang paling umum adalah Lámfada (\"tangan panjang\") dan Samildánach (\"sama mahirnya dalam banyak seni\"), dan perayaan panen Lughnasadh menyandang namanya.",
      "en": "Lugh or Lug (modern Irish: Lú) is a figure of Irish mythology. One of the Tuatha Dé Danann, a group of supernatural beings, he appears as a warrior, a king, a master craftsman and a saviour, skilled in many disciplines including the arts. He is also tied to oaths, truth and law, and so to rightful kingship. His best-known epithets are Lámfada (\"long hand\") and Samildánach (\"equally skilled in many arts\"), and the harvest festival of Lughnasadh bears his name.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lugh-c01",
        "lugh-c02",
        "lugh-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi, Lugh adalah putra Cian dan Ethniu, dan cucu dari pihak ibu sang tiran Fomoria Balor, yang ia bunuh dalam pertempuran Mag Tuired; putranya adalah pahlawan Cú Chulainn. Ia memiliki tombak berapi yang tak terhentikan, batu umban, dan anjing bernama Failinis, dan dikatakan menciptakan fidchell, permainan bola, dan pacuan kuda. Ia adalah wujud Irlandia dari dewa pan-Kelt Lugus, padanannya di Wales adalah Lleu Llaw Gyffes, dan dalam interpretatio romana ia disamakan dengan Mercury.",
      "en": "In myth Lugh is the son of Cian and Ethniu and the maternal grandson of the Fomorian tyrant Balor, whom he kills at the Battle of Mag Tuired; his son is the hero Cú Chulainn. He owns an unstoppable fiery spear, a sling stone and a hound named Failinis, and is credited with inventing fidchell, ball games and horse racing. He is the Irish form of the pan-Celtic god Lugus, his Welsh counterpart is Lleu Llaw Gyffes, and the interpretatio romana equates him with Mercury.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lugh-c04",
        "lugh-c05",
        "lugh-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel id, Lugus (Lugh) adalah dewa utama bangsa Kelt kuno dan raja Tuatha Dé Danann; ia memakai tombak petir Assal, dan ia punya dua kediaman, di Moytura (County Sligo) dan di Tara (County Meath). Orang Kelt kuno memperingati 1 Agustus sebagai Lughnasa, hari suci baginya. Arti namanya masih diperdebatkan: sebagian ahli menurunkannya dari akar kata Proto-Indo-Eropa \"mengikat dengan sumpah\", sedangkan tafsiran lama \"cahaya berkilat\" yang menjadikannya dewa matahari kini kebanyakan ditolak.",
      "en": "According to the id article, Lugus (Lugh) is the chief god of the ancient Celts and king of the Tuatha Dé Danann; he wields the lightning spear Assal and dwells at Tara in County Meath and Moytura in County Sligo. The ancient Celts marked 1 August as Lughnasa, a day holy to him. The meaning of his name is debated: some derive it from a Proto-Indo-European root \"to bind by oath\", while the older reading \"flashing light\", which made him a sun god, is now mostly rejected.",

      "quote": "is a figure in Irish mythology. A member of the Tuatha Dé Danann, a group of supernatural beings, Lugh is portrayed as a warrior, a king, a master craftsman and a saviour. He is associated with skill and mastery in multiple disciplines, including the arts.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

        "en": "Lugh is a figure of Irish mythology, one of the Tuatha Dé Danann, a warrior, king, master craftsman and saviour skilled in many arts."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lugh-c02",
      "source_id": "lugh-s1",
      "quote": "Lugh also has associations with oaths, truth, and the law, and therefore with rightful kingship. He is also associated with intelligence. Lugh is linked with the harvest festival of Lughnasadh, which bears his name.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

    {
      "id": "lugh-c03",
      "source_id": "lugh-s1",
      "quote": "His most common epithets are Lámfada (Modern Irish: Lámhfhada [ˈl̪ˠaːw ad̪ˠə] ; \"long hand\" or \"long arm\", possibly for his skill with a spear or his ability as a ruler) and Samildánach",

      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lugh-c04",
      "source_id": "lugh-s1",
      "quote": "In mythology, Lugh is the son of Cian and Ethniu (or Ethliu). He is the maternal grandson of the Fomorian tyrant Balor, whom Lugh kills in the Battle of Mag Tuired. Lugh's son is the hero Cú Chulainn, who is believed to be an incarnation of Lugh.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lugh putra Cian dan Ethniu, cucu tiran Fomoria (Fomorian) Balor yang ia bunuh dalam Battle of Mag Tuired; putranya pahlawan Cú Chulainn.",
        "en": "Lugh is son of Cian and Ethniu, grandson of the Fomorian tyrant Balor whom he kills at the Battle of Mag Tuired; his son is the hero Cú Chulainn."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lugh-c05",
      "source_id": "lugh-s1",
      "quote": "He wields an unstoppable fiery spear and a sling stone and owns a hound named Failinis. He is said to have invented fidchell, ball games, and horse racing.",

      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

    {
      "id": "lugh-c06",
      "source_id": "lugh-s1",
      "quote": "He is the Irish manifestation of the pan-Celtic god Lugus, and his Welsh counterpart is Lleu Llaw Gyffes. The interpretatio romana has Lug correspond to the Romans god Mercury.",

      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

    },
    {
      "id": "lugh-c07",
      "source_id": "lugh-s2",
      "quote": "menurut mitos celtic adalah dewa utama bagi Bangsa Celtic kuno, raja penguasa Tuatha Dé Danann. Dalam beberapa catatan oleh Julius Caesar, ia disandingkan dengan Mercury, dewa utama dalam mitologi Romawi",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

        "en": "In Celtic myth Lugh is the chief god of the ancient Celts (Bangsa Celtic) and king of the Tuatha Dé Danann; Julius Caesar paired him with Mercury (Romawi)."

    {

      "source_id": "lugh-s2",
      "quote": "Ia mempunyai dua tempat tinggal yang berada di Tara di County Meath dan di Moytura, di County Sligo.",

    {

      "quote": "Bangsa Celtic kuno memperingati setiap tanggal 1 Agustus yang disebut dengan Lughnasa, yang diyakini mereka sebagai hari suci untuk Dewa Laugh.",

        "en": "The ancient Celts (Bangsa Celtic) marked 1 August (Agustus) as Lughnasa, a holy day for the god (Dewa) Lugh."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Belenus (Belenos) adalah dewa penyembuh Kelt kuno, dipuja terutama di Aquileia dan Noricum, yang dalam sumber Romawi disamakan dengan Apollo.",
    "en": "Belenus (Belenos) is an ancient Celtic healing god, worshipped chiefly at Aquileia and in Noricum, and equated with Apollo in Roman sources.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "belenus-c01",
      "belenus-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Belenus (Galia: Belenos, Belinos) adalah dewa penyembuh Kelt (Celtic) kuno yang pemujaannya tercatat di sebagian besar dunia berbahasa Kelt. Pusat pemujaan utamanya di Aquileia, Italia timur laut, dan ia terutama dikaitkan dengan wilayah Noricum, tetapi namanya juga ditemukan dari Italia sampai Kepulauan Britania, termasuk Galia, Aquitania, dan Britania. Dalam sumber dan prasasti Romawi ia biasa disamakan dengan Apollo, terutama dalam kaitan dengan penyembuhan dan mata air berkhasiat.",
      "en": "Known in Gaulish as Belenos or Belinos, Belenus was an old Celtic god of healing, worshipped across much of the Celtic-speaking world. His main sanctuary was at Aquileia in north-eastern Italy, and he is chiefly tied to the Noricum region, yet references to him reach as far as Gaul, Aquitania and Britain in the north-west. Roman sources and inscriptions usually equate him with Apollo, especially where healing and curative springs are concerned.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "belenus-c01",
        "belenus-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Penulis kuno seperti Tertullian dan Herodian menggambarkannya sebagai dewa pelindung kota, terutama di Aquileia, tempat ia dianggap dewa pelindung kota itu; bukti arkeologi dan prasasti mengaitkan pemujaannya dengan air berkhasiat, tempat suci, dan di beberapa daerah fungsi orakel. Namanya muncul dalam sekitar 51 prasasti, kebanyakan di Aquileia. Menurut artikel de, sesudah Epona, Belenus adalah dewa Kelt yang paling banyak disebut penulis kuno; asal namanya diperdebatkan, antara \"yang bersinar\", \"dewa mata air\", atau kata Kelt untuk tanaman bilsenkraut (henbane).",
      "en": "Ancient writers such as Tertullian and Herodian describe him as a protective civic god, above all at Aquileia, where he was the city's patron; archaeology and inscriptions tie his cult to healing waters, sanctuaries and, in some places, oracles. His name appears in about 51 inscriptions, mostly at Aquileia. According to the de article, after Epona he is the Celtic god most often named by ancient authors; the origin of his name is disputed, between \"shining one\", \"spring god\", or the Celtic word for the plant henbane.",

        "belenus-c04",
        "belenus-c05",
        "belenus-c06",
        "belenus-c07"
      ]

      "quote": "is an ancient Celtic healing god whose cult is attested across much of the Celtic-speaking world. While his principal centre of worship lay at Aquileia in northeastern Italy, and the deity is primarily associated with the Noricum region, mentions extend from the Italian peninsula to the British Isles, including Gaul, Aquitania, and Britain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Belenus adalah dewa penyembuh Kelt (Celtic) kuno, berpusat di Aquileia, Italia (Italy) timur laut, terkait Noricum, dan disebut dari Italia sampai Kepulauan Britania (British Isles), Galia (Gaul), Aquitania, dan Britania (Britain).",
        "en": "Belenus is an ancient Celtic healing god centred at Aquileia in north-eastern Italy, tied to Noricum, attested from Italy to the British Isles, Gaul, Aquitania and Britain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "belenus-c02",
      "source_id": "belenus-s1",
      "quote": "In Roman sources and inscriptions, Belenus was commonly identified with Apollo through interpretatio romana, especially in contexts associated with healing and therapeutic springs.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sumber Romawi (Roman), Belenus disamakan dengan Apollo, terutama terkait penyembuhan dan mata air berkhasiat.",
        "en": "In Roman sources Belenus was identified with Apollo, especially for healing and therapeutic springs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "belenus-c03",
      "source_id": "belenus-s1",
      "quote": "Ancient authors such as Tertullian and Herodian describe him as a prominent protective and civic deity, notably at Aquileia, where he was regarded as the city's patron god. Archaeological and epigraphic evidence further indicate that his cult was closely connected with medicinal waters, sanctuaries, and, in some regions, oracular functions.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tertullian dan Herodian menyebutnya dewa pelindung kota, terutama di Aquileia; pemujaannya terkait air berkhasiat, tempat suci, dan orakel.",
        "en": "Tertullian and Herodian call him a protective civic deity, notably at Aquileia; his cult tied to medicinal waters, sanctuaries and oracles."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "belenus-c04",
      "source_id": "belenus-s1",
      "quote": "which is a latinised form of the Gaulish Belenos (or Belinos), appears in some 51 inscriptions. Although most of them are located in Aquileia (Friuli, Italy), the main centre of his cult",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Belenus, bentuk Latin dari Belenos (Gaulish, Galia), muncul dalam sekitar 51 prasasti, kebanyakan di Aquileia (Friuli, Italia).",
        "en": "Belenus, the Latin form of Gaulish Belenos, appears in some 51 inscriptions, mostly at Aquileia (Friuli, Italy)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "belenus-c05",
      "source_id": "belenus-s1",
      "quote": "Traditionally, the name has been interpreted as meaning 'bright one' or the 'shining one'",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Secara tradisional namanya diartikan \"yang cerah\" atau \"yang bersinar\" (shining one).",
        "en": "Traditionally the name is read as 'bright one' or 'shining one'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "belenus-c06",
      "source_id": "belenus-s2",
      "quote": "Belenus, latinisierte Form von Belenos, Belinos, war ein keltischer Gott, der nach der Interpretatio Romana mit dem römischen Apollon gleichgesetzt wurde. Nach Epona ist er die von antiken Autoren am meisten genannte keltische Gottheit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Belenus (Belenos, Belinos) adalah dewa Kelt (keltischer Gott) yang disamakan dengan Apollon Romawi (römischen); sesudah Epona ia dewa Kelt yang paling banyak disebut penulis kuno (antiken Autoren).",
        "en": "Belenus (Belenos, Belinos) was a Celtic god (keltischer Gott) equated with the Roman (römischen) Apollon; after Epona he is the Celtic deity most named by ancient authors (antiken Autoren)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "belenus-c07",
      "source_id": "belenus-s2",
      "quote": "Für die Deutung von Belenus als Heilgottheit spricht die Etymologie als „Quellgott“",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Etimologi sebagai dewa mata air (Quellgott) mendukung tafsiran Belenus sebagai dewa penyembuh (Heilgottheit).",
        "en": "The etymology as a spring god (Quellgott) supports reading Belenus as a healing deity (Heilgottheit)."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kelpie adalah roh mitos berubah wujud dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia yang menghuni danau, biasanya berwujud kuda kelabu atau putih yang dapat menjadi manusia.",
    "en": "The kelpie is a mythical shapeshifting spirit of Scottish folklore that haunts lochs, usually a grey or white horse able to take human form.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kelpie-c01",
      "kelpie-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish folklore), kelpie atau water kelpie adalah roh mitos yang dapat berubah wujud dan menghuni loch (danau) di Skotlandia; ia juga menghuni danau kecil dan sungai dalam mitologi Yorkshire. Legenda kuda air berubah wujud dengan berbagai nama tersebar di seluruh Kepulauan Britania. Kelpie biasanya digambarkan sebagai makhluk mirip kuda berwarna kelabu atau putih yang dapat berwujud manusia; sebagian kisah menyebut ia tetap berkuku saat menjadi manusia, sehingga dikaitkan dengan Setan, seperti disinggung Robert Burns dalam puisi \"Address to the Devil\" (1786).",
      "en": "In Scottish folklore the kelpie, or water kelpie, is a mythical shapeshifting spirit living in Scotland's lochs; it also haunts meres and streams in Yorkshire mythology. Tales of shape-changing water horses under many names spread across the British Isles. The kelpie is usually a grey or white horse-like creature that can take human form; some accounts say it keeps its hooves in human shape, which linked it with Satan, as Robert Burns hints in his 1786 poem \"Address to the Devil\".",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kelpie-c01",
        "kelpie-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Hampir setiap perairan besar di Skotlandia punya kisah kelpie, tetapi yang paling banyak dilaporkan adalah di Loch Ness. Padanannya di dunia antara lain nixie Jermanik, wihwin dari Amerika Tengah, dan bunyip Australia. Kisah-kisah ini secara praktis berguna untuk menjauhkan anak-anak dari perairan berbahaya dan memperingatkan perempuan muda agar waspada terhadap orang asing yang tampan. Asal kata Skots kelpie tidak pasti, mungkin dari bahasa Gaelik calpa atau cailpeach (\"sapi dara\" atau \"anak kuda\"); penggunaan pertamanya untuk makhluk mitos, dengan ejaan kaelpie, terdapat dalam ode William Collins sebelum 1759.",
      "en": "Nearly every sizeable body of water in Scotland has a kelpie tale, but Loch Ness is the most reported. Similar beings elsewhere include the bunyip of Australia, the wihwin of Central America and the nixie of the Germanic peoples. Such stories served to keep children from dangerous waters and to warn young women about handsome strangers. The origin of the Scots word kelpie is unsure, perhaps from Gaelic calpa or cailpeach (\"heifer\" or \"colt\"); its first use for the mythical creature, spelled kaelpie, is in an ode by William Collins written before 1759.",

      "en": "The kelpie is the most common water spirit of Scottish folklore. According to the id article it appears in art and literature, including two 30-metre steel sculptures at Falkirk, The Kelpies, finished in October 2013.",
