  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Manannán atau Manann, juga Manannán mac Lir (\"anak laut\"), adalah dewa laut, pejuang, dan raja dunia lain dalam mitologi Gaelik (Gaelic) Irlandia, Man, dan Skotlandia, dan salah satu Tuatha Dé Danann. Ia penguasa dan penjaga dunia lain, yang disebut Emain Ablach (\"Pulau Pohon Apel\"), Mag Mell (\"Dataran Kebahagiaan\"), atau Tír Tairngire (\"Negeri Perjanjian\"). Setelah kedatangan manusia (kaum Milesia), ia menjadi raja atas Tuatha Dé yang tersisa dan memakai kabut gaib (féth fíada) untuk menyembunyikan kediamannya dan sidhe yang lain.",
      "en": "Manannán (or Manann), called Manannán mac Lir, 'son of the Sea', is the sea god, warrior and otherworld king of Irish, Manx and Scottish Gaelic myth, and belongs to the Tuatha Dé Danann. As lord and keeper of the otherworld, his realm goes by several names: Tír Tairngire, the 'Land of Promise'; Mag Mell, the 'Plain of Delights'; and Emain Ablach, the 'Isle of Apple Trees'. After the coming of humans (the Milesians) he became over-king of the remaining Tuatha Dé and uses the mist of invisibility (féth fíada) to hide his home and the other sidhe.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "manannan-mac-lir-c01",
        "manannan-mac-lir-c02",
        "manannan-mac-lir-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia memiliki perahu yang berlayar sendiri, Sguaba Tuinne (\"Penyapu Ombak\"), kuda Aonbharr yang dapat berlari di atas air dan darat, dan pedang mematikan Fragarach yang menguras kekuatan. Dalam legenda Skotlandia dan Man ia dikenal sebagai Manannan beg mac y Leir, dan Isle of Man (Mannin) umumnya dianggap dinamai menurutnya; ia sekerabat dengan tokoh Wales Manawydan fab Llŷr. Namanya dieja Manandán dalam bahasa Irlandia Kuno dan Mannan dalam bahasa Man. Menurut artikel id, ia dipandang sebagai penjaga dunia lain dan salah satu yang mengantar jiwa ke alam baka, dan juga dikenal sebagai Oirbsiu atau Oirbsen, asal nama Lough Corrib.",
      "en": "He owns a self-sailing boat, Sguaba Tuinne ('Wave-sweeper'), a horse, Aonbharr, that gallops across sea and land alike, and Fragarach, a lethal sword that drains an enemy's strength. In Scottish and Manx legend he is Manannan beg mac y Leir, and the Isle of Man (Mannin) is generally held to be named after him; he is cognate with the Welsh figure Manawydan fab Llŷr. His name is Manandán in Old Irish and Mannan in Manx. According to the id article he is seen as guardian of the otherworld and one who brings souls to the afterlife, and is also known as Oirbsiu or Oirbsen, from whom Lough Corrib takes its name.",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c01",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s1",
      "quote": "Manannán or Manann , also known as Manannán mac Lir ('son of the Sea'), is a sea god, warrior and king of the otherworld in Gaelic (Irish, Manx and Scottish) mythology who is one of the Tuatha Dé Danann .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manannán (Manann, Manannán mac Lir, son of the Sea) adalah dewa laut, pejuang, dan raja dunia lain mitologi Gaelik (Gaelic: Irish, Manx, Scottish), salah satu Tuatha Dé Danann.",
        "en": "Manannán (Manann, Manannán mac Lir, son of the Sea) is a sea god, warrior and otherworld king of Gaelic (Irish, Manx, Scottish) myth, one of the Tuatha Dé Danann."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c02",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s1",
      "quote": "He is seen as a ruler and guardian of the otherworld and his dominion is referred by such names as Emain Ablach (or Emhain Abhlach , 'Isle of Apple Trees'), Mag Mell ('Plain of Delights'), or Tír Tairngire ('Land of Promise').",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia penguasa dan penjaga dunia lain: Emain Ablach (Pulau Pohon Apel, Isle of Apple Trees), Mag Mell (Dataran Kebahagiaan), Tír Tairngire (Negeri Perjanjian).",
        "en": "He rules and guards the otherworld: Emain Ablach (Isle of Apple Trees), Mag Mell (Plain of Delights), Tír Tairngire (Land of Promise)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c03",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s1",
      "quote": "He is said to own a self-navigating boat named Sguaba Tuinne ('Wave-sweeper'), a horse Aonbharr which can course over water as well as land and a deadly strength-sapping sword named Fragarach",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia memiliki perahu Sguaba Tuinne (Wave-sweeper, Penyapu Ombak), kuda Aonbharr yang berlari di air dan darat, dan pedang Fragarach.",
        "en": "He owns the boat Sguaba Tuinne (Wave-sweeper), the horse Aonbharr that runs over water and land, and the sword Fragarach."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c04",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s1",
      "quote": "Manannán appears also in Scottish and Manx legend, where he is known as Manannan beg mac y Leir ('little Manannan, son of the Sea'). The Isle of Man (Mannin ) is generally thought to be named after him, though some have said he is named after the island. He is cognate with the Welsh figure Manawydan fab Llŷr .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda Skotlandia dan Man (Scottish, Manx) ia Manannan beg mac y Leir; Isle of Man (Mannin) dinamai menurutnya; sekerabat dengan Manawydan fab Llŷr (Welsh, Wales).",
        "en": "In Scottish and Manx legend he is Manannan beg mac y Leir; the Isle of Man (Mannin) is named after him; cognate with the Welsh Manawydan fab Llŷr."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c05",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s1",
      "quote": "His name is spelt Manandán in Old Irish, Manannán in Modern Irish, Manannàn in Scottish Gaelic and Mannan in Manx Gaelic.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya Manandán (Old Irish, Irlandia Kuno), Manannán (Modern Irish), dan Mannan (Manx Gaelic, bahasa Man).",
        "en": "His name is Manandán in Old Irish, Manannán in Modern Irish and Mannan in Manx Gaelic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c06",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s2",
      "quote": "Ia dipandang sebagai 'the guardian of the Otherworld (penjaga dunia lain) dan salah satu yang membebaskan jiwa-jiwa ke alam baka.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manannán dipandang sebagai penjaga dunia lain (guardian of the Otherworld) dan pembebas jiwa ke alam baka.",
        "en": "Manannán is seen as the guardian of the Otherworld (penjaga dunia lain) who frees souls into the afterlife (alam baka)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c07",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s2",
      "quote": "Manannán juga dikenal sebagai Oirbsiu atau Oirbsen, asal Lough Corrib mengambil namanya.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manannán juga dikenal sebagai Oirbsiu atau Oirbsen, asal nama Lough Corrib.",
        "en": "Manannán is also known as Oirbsiu or Oirbsen, from whom Lough Corrib takes its name."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c08",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s2",
      "quote": "Manannán selanjutnya diidentifikasikan dengan sosok penipu dalam Bodach an Chóta Lachtna",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manannán diidentifikasikan dengan sosok penipu dalam Bodach an Chóta Lachtna.",
        "en": "Manannán is identified with the trickster (sosok penipu) in Bodach an Chóta Lachtna."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "manannan-mac-lir-c09",
      "source_id": "manannan-mac-lir-s1",
      "quote": "He is described as over-king of the surviving Tuatha Dé after the advent of humans (Milesians) and uses the mist of invisibility (féth fíada ) to cloak the whereabouts of his home as well as the sidhe dwellings of the others.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah kedatangan manusia (Milesians, Milesia), Manannán raja atas Tuatha Dé yang tersisa dan memakai kabut gaib (féth fíada) untuk menyembunyikan kediamannya.",
        "en": "After the coming of humans (Milesians) Manannán is over-king of the surviving Tuatha Dé and uses the féth fíada mist to hide his home."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Naga Wales (Y Ddraig Goch, \"naga merah\") adalah simbol heraldik Wales yang tampil pada bendera nasionalnya, berakar pada kisah naga merah yang bertarung melawan naga putih di Dinas Emrys.",
    "en": "The Welsh Dragon (Y Ddraig Goch, 'the red dragon') is the heraldic symbol of Wales on its national flag, rooted in the tale of a red dragon fighting a white dragon at Dinas Emrys.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "welsh-dragon-c01",
      "welsh-dragon-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Naga Wales (bahasa Wales: y Ddraig Goch, \"naga merah\") adalah simbol heraldik yang mewakili Wales dan tampil pada bendera nasionalnya. Para pemimpin kuno Briton Kelt, seperti Maelgwn Gwynedd, Mynyddog Mwynfawr, dan Urien Rheged, dipersonifikasikan sebagai naga, begitu pula pemimpin Wales kemudian seperti Owain Gwynedd, Llywelyn ap Gruffydd, dan Owain Glyndŵr.",
      "en": "The Welsh Dragon (Welsh: y Ddraig Goch, 'the red dragon') is a heraldic symbol representing Wales and shown on its national flag. Ancient leaders of the Celtic Britons such as Maelgwn Gwynedd, Mynyddog Mwynfawr and Urien Rheged were personified as dragons, as were later Welsh leaders such as Owain Gwynedd, Llywelyn ap Gruffydd and Owain Glyndŵr.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "welsh-dragon-c01",
        "welsh-dragon-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Naga merah muncul dalam kisah Mabinogion tentang Lludd dan Llefelys, terkurung dan bertarung melawan naga putih penyerbu di Dinas Emrys. Dalam Historia Brittonum (sekitar 829 M), Gwrtheyrn, raja Briton, gagal membangun benteng di Dinas Emrys; seorang anak bernama Emrys menyuruhnya menggali dua naga yang bertarung di bawah benteng, dan naga putih yang melambangkan Anglo-Saxon akan segera dikalahkan naga merah Wales. Bendera dengan naga merah menjadi bendera resmi Wales pada 1959. Pemakaian istilah naga (draco) untuk militer berasal dari zaman Romawi, dan panji draco mungkin memengaruhi naga Wales.",
      "en": "The red dragon appears in the Mabinogion tale of Lludd and Llefelys, imprisoned and fighting an invading white dragon at Dinas Emrys. In the Historia Brittonum (c. 829 AD), Gwrtheyrn, king of the Britons, fails to build a fort at Dinas Emrys; a boy named Emrys tells him to dig up two dragons fighting beneath it, and the white dragon of the Anglo-Saxons is soon to be beaten by the red dragon of Wales. The flag with the red dragon became Wales's official flag in 1959. Military use of the word dragon (draco) goes back to Roman times, and the draco standard may have influenced the Welsh dragon.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "welsh-dragon-c02",

      "id": "Menurut artikel id, naga merah sering dianggap standar perang Raja Arthur dan pemimpin Kelt kuno lain; pada masa raja-raja Tudor naga merah dipakai sebagai pendukung lambang Kerajaan Inggris, dan kini dipakai banyak lembaga Wales, termasuk Pemerintah Welsh dan Senedd.",
      "en": "According to the id article the red dragon is popularly seen as the battle standard of King Arthur and other ancient Celtic leaders; under the Tudor kings it was used as a supporter in the royal arms of England, and today many Welsh institutions use it, including the Welsh Government and the Senedd.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Imp adalah makhluk mitologi Eropa yang mirip peri atau iblis, biasanya kecil, lincah, dan lebih usil daripada berbahaya.",
    "en": "In European myth the imp is a small, lively being, akin to a fairy or a demon, that is more of a nuisance than a real danger.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "imp-c01",
      "imp-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Imp adalah makhluk mitologi Eropa (European) yang mirip peri atau iblis, sering digambarkan dalam cerita rakyat dan takhayul. Imp biasanya digambarkan merepotkan dan usil, bukan ancaman serius, dan dianggap makhluk rendahan; para pengiring Iblis kadang disebut imp. Mereka biasanya lincah dan bertubuh kecil. Berbeda dengan cerita rakyat Kristen, iblis dalam legenda Jermanik tidak selalu jahat, dan imp sering lebih usil daripada berbahaya; dalam sebagian kepercayaan mereka pelayan para dewa.",
      "en": "Folklore and superstition often speak of the imp, a being somewhere between a fairy and a demon. Imps are usually troublesome and mischievous rather than seriously dangerous, and are counted as lesser beings; the devil's attendants are sometimes called imps. They are usually lively and small. Unlike in Christian folklore, demons in Germanic legend were not always evil, and imps were often mischievous rather than harmful; in some religions they served the gods.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "imp-c01",
        "imp-c02",
        "imp-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kata benda impa dalam bahasa Inggris Kuno berarti tunas muda tanaman, kemudian keturunan keluarga bangsawan atau anak; sejak abad ke-16 dipakai dalam ungkapan seperti \"imp of hell\" dan \"imp of the devil\", dan pada abad ke-17 berarti iblis kecil, familiar seorang penyihir. Legenda Lincolnshire dari abad ke-14 mengisahkan Iblis yang kesal dengan selesainya katedral Lincoln datang bersama dua imp yang merusak gedung itu; seorang malaikat menyuruh mereka berhenti, dan imp yang melempar batu kepadanya seketika membatu. Lincoln Imp kini menjadi simbol kota itu.",
      "en": "The Old English noun impa meant a young shoot of a plant, later the scion of a noble house or a child; from the 16th century it appears in phrases like \"imp of hell\" and \"imp of the devil\", and by the 17th century it meant a small demon, a witch's familiar. A 14th-century Lincolnshire legend tells how the devil, vexed at the finished Lincoln Cathedral, came with two imps who wrecked the building; an angel ordered them to stop, and the imp that threw a stone at it was turned to stone on the spot. The Lincoln Imp is now a symbol of the city.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "imp-c03",
        "imp-c05",
        "imp-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "quote": "An imp is a European mythological being similar to a fairy or demon, frequently described in folklore and superstition. The word may perhaps derive from the term ympe, used to denote a young grafted tree.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Imp adalah makhluk mitologi Eropa (European) mirip peri atau iblis, dalam cerita rakyat dan takhayul; katanya mungkin dari ympe.",
        "en": "An imp is a European mythological being like a fairy or demon in folklore and superstition; the word may come from ympe."
      }

    {
      "id": "imp-c02",
      "source_id": "imp-s1",
      "quote": "Imps are often described as troublesome and mischievous more than seriously threatening or dangerous, and as lesser beings rather than more important supernatural beings.       "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Imp usil, bukan ancaman serius, makhluk rendahan, kadang pengiring Iblis, lincah dan kecil.",
        "en": "Imps are mischievous rather than dangerous, lesser beings, sometimes the devil's attendants, lively and small."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "imp-c03",
      "source_id": "imp-s1",
      "quote": "The Old English noun impa meant a young shoot or scion of a plant or tree, and later came to mean the scion of a noble house, or a child in general.       "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Impa (Old English, Inggris Kuno) berarti tunas muda, lalu keturunan bangsawan atau anak; sejak abad ke-16 \"imp of hell\", dan pada abad ke-17 iblis kecil, familiar penyihir.",
        "en": "Old English impa meant a young shoot, then a noble scion or child; from the 16th century \"imp of hell\", and by the 17th a small demon, a witch's familiar."

      "source_id": "imp-s1",
      "quote": "Unlike the Christian folklore, demons in Germanic legends were not necessarily always evil. Imps were often mischievous rather than evil or harmful. In some religions, they were attendants of the gods.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Brownie adalah roh rumah atau hobgoblin dari cerita rakyat Skotlandia yang keluar malam hari untuk mengerjakan tugas rumah dan pertanian, dengan imbalan semangkuk susu atau krim.",
    "en": "The brownie is a household spirit or hobgoblin of Scottish folklore that comes out at night to do household and farm chores in return for a bowl of milk or cream.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "brownie-folklore-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Brownie atau broonie (Scots), juga brùnaidh atau gruagach (Scottish Gaelic), adalah roh rumah atau hobgoblin dari cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish folklore) yang konon keluar pada malam hari ketika pemilik rumah tidur untuk mengerjakan berbagai tugas rumah dan pertanian. Pemilik rumah harus meninggalkan semangkuk susu, krim, atau persembahan lain, biasanya di dekat perapian. Brownie mudah tersinggung dan akan pergi selamanya bila merasa dihina atau dimanfaatkan; mereka juga usil dan suka menghukum pelayan yang malas.",
      "en": "The brownie or broonie (Scots), also brùnaidh or gruagach (Scottish Gaelic), is a household spirit or hobgoblin of Scottish folklore said to come out at night while the household sleeps and do chores and farm work. The owners must leave a bowl of milk, cream or some other offering, usually by the hearth. Brownies take offence easily and leave forever if they feel insulted or used; they are also mischievous and like to punish lazy servants.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "brownie-folklore-c01",
        "brownie-folklore-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Brownie berasal dari roh pelindung rumah yang sangat mirip Lares dalam tradisi Romawi kuno. Mereka biasanya digambarkan jelek, berkulit cokelat, dan berbulu; dalam kisah tertua berukuran manusia atau lebih besar, belakangan kecil dan keriput. Mereka sering dapat menjadi tak kasatmata dan kadang berwujud hewan, selalu telanjang atau berpakaian compang-camping, dan akan pergi selamanya bila diberi pakaian atau dibaptis. Varian regionalnya antara lain hob dan ùruisg, sedangkan di luar Inggris dan Skotlandia ada Bwbach dari Wales dan Fenodyree dari Man.",
      "en": "Brownies began as domestic guardian spirits very like the Lares of ancient Roman tradition. They are usually described as ugly, brown-skinned and hairy; in the oldest tales human-sized or bigger, more recently small and wizened. They can often turn invisible and sometimes take animal shapes, are always naked or in rags, and leave forever if given clothes or baptized. Regional variants include hobs and ùruisgs, while beyond England and Scotland there are the Welsh Bwbach and the Manx Fenodyree.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "brownie-folklore-c03",
        "brownie-folklore-c04",
        "brownie-folklore-c05",
        "brownie-folklore-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel id, brownie adalah sejenis peri atau hobgoblin kecil yang rajin, mendiami rumah atau lumbung, jarang terlihat tetapi sering terdengar membersihkan di malam hari, dan mengharapkan hadiah makanan seperti bubur dan madu sebagai tanda penghargaan, bukan bayaran. Brownie juga muncul dalam puisi L'Allegro karya John Milton, dan kelompok Brownies dalam Girl Guides dinamai menurut cerita Juliana Horatia Ewing.",
      "en": "According to the id article, the brownie is a small, hard-working fairy or hobgoblin living in a house or barn, seldom seen but often heard cleaning at night, and expecting gifts of food such as porridge and honey as a token of thanks, not payment. Brownies also appear in John Milton's poem L'Allegro, and the Brownies of the Girl Guides are named after a story by Juliana Horatia Ewing.",

        "brownie-folklore-c06",
        "brownie-folklore-c07",
        "brownie-folklore-c08",
        "brownie-folklore-c09"

      "quote": "A brownie or broonie (Scots), also known as a brùnaidh or gruagach (Scottish Gaelic), is a household spirit or hobgoblin from Scottish folklore that is said to come out at night while the owners of the house are asleep and perform various chores and farming tasks. The human owners of the house must leave a bowl of milk or cream or some other offering for the brownie, usually by the hearth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie (broonie, Scots; brùnaidh, gruagach, Scottish Gaelic) adalah roh rumah atau hobgoblin cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish folklore) yang bekerja malam hari, diberi semangkuk susu atau krim di dekat perapian.",
        "en": "The brownie (broonie; brùnaidh, gruagach) is a household spirit or hobgoblin of Scottish folklore working at night, given a bowl of milk or cream by the hearth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c02",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Brownies are described as easily offended and will leave their homes forever if they feel they have been insulted or in any way taken advantage of. Brownies are characteristically mischievous and are often said to punish or pull pranks on lazy servants.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie mudah tersinggung, pergi selamanya bila dihina, usil, dan menghukum pelayan malas.",
        "en": "Brownies are easily offended, leave forever if insulted, are mischievous and punish lazy servants."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c03",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Brownies originated as domestic tutelary spirits, very similar to the Lares of ancient Roman tradition.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie berasal dari roh pelindung rumah, mirip Lares tradisi Romawi (Roman) kuno.",
        "en": "Brownies began as domestic tutelary spirits, like the Lares of ancient Roman tradition."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c04",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "they are usually described as ugly, brown-skinned, and covered in hair. In the oldest stories, they are usually human-sized or larger. In more recent times, they have come to be seen as small and wizened. They are often capable of turning invisible, and they sometimes appear in the shapes of animals.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie jelek, berkulit cokelat, berbulu; dulu berukuran manusia, kini kecil; dapat tak kasatmata dan berwujud hewan; pergi bila diberi pakaian atau dibaptis.",
        "en": "Brownies are ugly, brown-skinned, hairy; once human-sized, now small; can turn invisible and take animal shapes; leave if given clothes or baptized."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c05",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Regional variants in England and Scotland include hobs, and ùruisgs. Variants outside England and Scotland are the Welsh Bwbach and the Manx Fenodyree.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Varian di Inggris dan Skotlandia (England, Scotland): hob dan ùruisg; di luar itu Bwbach (Welsh, Wales) dan Fenodyree (Manx, Man).",
        "en": "Variants in England and Scotland include hobs and ùruisgs; outside them the Welsh Bwbach and Manx Fenodyree."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c06",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Brownie merupakan sejenis peri atau hobgoblin kecil yang rajin, dan dipercayai mendiami suatu rumah atau lumbung. Mereka jarang terlihat, tetapi sering terdengar di malam hari, membersihkan dan melakukan pekerjaan rumah. Sebagai imbalan atas kerja kerasnya, Brownie mengharapkan hadiah makanan, seperti bubur dan madu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie peri atau hobgoblin kecil yang rajin di rumah atau lumbung, jarang terlihat, terdengar bekerja malam hari, dan mengharapkan bubur dan madu.",
        "en": "The Brownie is a small hard-working fairy or hobgoblin in a house or barn, seldom seen, heard working at night, expecting porridge and honey (bubur, madu)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c07",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Brownie adalah makhluk mitologi yang populer dalam cerita rakyat di sekitar Skotlandia dan Inggris.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie makhluk mitologi populer dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia dan Inggris.",
        "en": "The Brownie is a mythological being popular in Scottish and English (Skotlandia, Inggris) folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c08",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "Brownies have also appeared outside of folklore, including in John Milton's poem L'Allegro. They became popular in works of children's literature in the late nineteenth century and continue to appear in works of modern fantasy. The Brownies in the Girl Guides are named after a short story by Juliana Horatia Ewing based on brownie folklore.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie muncul dalam puisi L'Allegro karya John Milton; Brownies dalam Girl Guides dinamai menurut cerita Juliana Horatia Ewing.",
        "en": "Brownies appear in John Milton's L'Allegro; the Brownies of the Girl Guides are named after a story by Juliana Horatia Ewing."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c09",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Namun mereka tidak menganggap hadiahnya tersebut sebagai pembayaran, tetapi hanya sebagai tanda penghargaan.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie tidak menganggap hadiah itu bayaran, hanya tanda penghargaan.",
        "en": "Brownies take the gift not as payment (pembayaran) but as a token of thanks (tanda penghargaan)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "brownie-folklore-c10",
      "source_id": "brownie-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "They are always either naked or dressed in rags. If a person attempts to present a brownie with clothing or baptize it, it will leave forever.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brownie selalu telanjang atau compang-camping, dan pergi selamanya bila diberi pakaian atau dibaptis.",
        "en": "Brownies are always naked or in rags, and leave forever if given clothing or baptized."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Selkie adalah makhluk mitologi yang dapat berubah wujud antara anjing laut dan manusia dengan melepas atau memakai kulit anjing lautnya, terutama dalam tradisi Kelt dan Nordik.",
    "en": "Selkies are mythological creatures that shift between seal and human form by taking off or putting on their sealskin, chiefly in Celtic and Norse tradition.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "selkie-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Selkie adalah makhluk mitologi yang dapat berubah wujud antara anjing laut dan manusia dengan melepas atau memakai kulit anjing lautnya. Mereka menonjol dalam tradisi lisan dan mitologi berbagai budaya, terutama Kelt dan Nordik (Celtic, Norse). Kata selkie berasal dari kata Skots untuk anjing laut, dan mereka kadang disebut selkie fowk, \"bangsa anjing laut\". Selkie terutama dikaitkan dengan Northern Isles Skotlandia, tempat mereka hidup sebagai anjing laut di laut tetapi melepas kulitnya untuk menjadi manusia di darat.",
      "en": "Selkies are mythological creatures that shapeshift between seal and human by removing or donning their sealskin. They feature in the oral traditions and mythology of many cultures, especially Celtic and Norse ones. The word selkie comes from the Scots word for seal, and they are sometimes called selkie fowk, \"seal folk\". They are chiefly tied to the Northern Isles of Scotland, where they live as seals in the sea but shed their skins to become human on land.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "selkie-c01",
        "selkie-c02",
        "selkie-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Selkie bersifat ganda: mereka bisa ramah dan menolong manusia, tetapi juga berbahaya dan pendendam. Dalam wujud manusia mereka sering menarik dan memikat, dan banyak kisah tentang hubungan cinta selkie dengan manusia yang kadang melahirkan anak. Selkie juga bisa dipaksa atau ditipu menikahi manusia, biasanya oleh orang yang mencuri dan menyembunyikan kulit anjing lautnya; pernikahan seperti itu sering tak bahagia, karena selkie selalu merindukan laut dan dapat kabur bila menemukan kulitnya.",
      "en": "Selkies have a double nature: they can be friendly and helpful to humans, but also dangerous and vengeful. In human form they are often attractive and seductive, and many tales tell of love affairs between selkies and humans, sometimes producing children. A selkie may also be forced or tricked into marrying a human, usually one who steals and hides its sealskin; such marriages are often unhappy, for the selkie always longs for the sea and may flee if it finds its skin.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "selkie-c04",
        "selkie-c05"

      "id": "Menurut artikel id, selkie ditemukan dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia, Skotlandia, dan Faroe, dan kisahnya sangat mirip dengan kisah gadis angsa.       "en": "According to the id article selkies are found in Irish, Scottish and Faroese folklore, and their tale is very like the swan-maiden story.   "short_description": {
    "id": "Pixie (juga pisky atau piskie) adalah makhluk mitos cerita rakyat Britania, terutama di dataran tinggi Cornwall dan Devon, yang kecil, usil, dan gemar menari semalaman.",
    "en": "The pixie (also pisky or piskie) is a mythical creature of British folklore, mainly of the Cornwall and Devon moorlands, small, mischievous and fond of dancing all night.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "pixie-c01",
      "pixie-c02",
      "pixie-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pixie (juga pisky, pixy, pixi, pizkie, piskie, atau pigsie di sebagian Cornwall dan Devon) adalah makhluk mitos cerita rakyat Britania (British folklore). Pixie diduga paling banyak di dataran tinggi sekitar Cornwall dan Devon, yang mengisyaratkan asal-usul Kelt bagi kepercayaan dan namanya, meski kata pixie juga dijumpai di Dorset, Somerset, dan beberapa daerah lain. Seperti Aos Sí Irlandia dan Skotlandia, pixie dipercaya mendiami situs bawah tanah kuno seperti lingkaran batu, barrow, dolmen, ringfort, atau menhir.",
      "en": "Known locally in Cornwall and Devon by names such as pisky, piskie or pigsie, the pixie is a mythical being of British folklore. Pixies are thought to cluster in the high moorland around Cornwall and Devon, hinting at a Celtic origin for the belief and the name, though the word also turns up in Dorset, Somerset and elsewhere. Like the Irish and Scottish Aos Sí, pixies are thought to dwell beneath old monuments: menhirs, dolmens, barrows, ringforts and stone circles.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pixie-c01",
        "pixie-c02",
        "pixie-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita tradisional, pixie umumnya tidak jahat, usil, bertubuh pendek, dan kekanak-kanakan; mereka suka menari dan bergulat di luar sepanjang malam. Di era modern mereka biasanya digambarkan bertelinga runcing, berpakaian hijau, dan bertopi runcing. Asal kata pixie tidak pasti; mungkin dari pyske dalam dialek Swedia atau dari akar Kelt untuk \"kecil\", dan secara bersama mereka disebut Pobel Vean (\"orang kecil\"). Di Penwith barat, spriggan dibedakan dari pixie karena sifatnya yang jahat.",
      "en": "In traditional lore pixies are generally harmless, mischievous, short and childlike, fond of dancing and wrestling outdoors through the night. In modern times they are usually shown with pointed ears, green clothes and a pointed hat. The origin of the word is unclear; perhaps Swedish dialect pyske or a Celtic root for \"little\", and collectively they are called Pobel Vean (\"little people\"). In west Penwith spriggans are told apart from pixies by their malevolence.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pixie-c04",
        "pixie-c05",
        "pixie-c06",
        "pixie-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel de, pixie adalah makhluk dongeng kecil dari cerita rakyat Inggris yang mirip kobold dan peri, dengan kisah yang tersebar di Devon dan Cornwall. Ada beberapa mitos asal-usul: mereka makhluk yang tak cukup baik untuk surga dan tak cukup jahat untuk neraka sehingga harus tinggal di bumi selamanya, atau para druid yang menolak masuk Kristen dan dikecilkan Tuhan sebagai hukuman.",
      "en": "According to the de article, pixies are small fabulous beings of English folklore resembling kobolds and fairies, with stories spread in Devon and Cornwall. There are several origin myths: they were beings not good enough for heaven nor bad enough for hell and so had to stay on earth forever, or druids who refused Christianity and were shrunk by God as punishment.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pixie-c07",
        "pixie-c08"
      ]
    }

      "quote": "A pixie (also called pisky, pixy, pixi, pizkie, piskie, or pigsie in parts of Cornwall and Devon) is a mythical creature of British folklore.",

      "id": "pixie-c02",
      "source_id": "pixie-s1",
      "quote": "Pixies are speculated to be particularly concentrated in the high moorland areas around Cornwall and Devon, suggesting some Celtic origin for the belief and name. However, the word 'pixie' (under various forms) also appears in Dorset, Somerset and to a lesser extent in Sussex, Wiltshire and Hampshire.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pixie terutama di dataran tinggi Cornwall dan Devon, mengisyaratkan asal Kelt (Celtic); kata pixie juga ada di Dorset, Somerset, dan lain-lain.",
        "en": "Pixies cluster in the moorlands of Cornwall and Devon, suggesting a Celtic origin; the word also appears in Dorset, Somerset and elsewhere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pixie-c03",
      "source_id": "pixie-s1",
      "quote": "Similar to the Irish and Scottish Aos Sí (also spelled Aos Sidhe), pixies are believed to inhabit ancient underground sites such as stone circles, barrows, dolmens, ringforts, or menhirs.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti Aos Sí Irlandia dan Skotlandia (Irish, Scottish), pixie mendiami situs bawah tanah kuno seperti lingkaran batu, barrow, dolmen, ringfort, menhir.",
        "en": "Like the Irish and Scottish Aos Sí, pixies inhabit ancient underground sites such as stone circles, barrows, dolmens, ringforts or menhirs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pixie-c04",
      "source_id": "pixie-s1",
      "quote": "In traditional regional lore, pixies are generally benign, mischievous, short of stature, and childlike; they are fond of dancing and wrestling outdoors, which they perform through the night.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pixie umumnya tak jahat, usil, pendek, kekanak-kanakan, suka menari dan bergulat sepanjang malam.",
        "en": "Pixies are generally benign, mischievous, short and childlike, dancing and wrestling outdoors through the night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pixie-c05",
      "source_id": "pixie-s1",
      "quote": "The term Pobel Vean (Little People) is often used to refer to them collectively.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebutan Pobel Vean (Little People, orang kecil) dipakai untuk mereka secara bersama.",
        "en": "The term Pobel Vean (Little People) is used for them collectively."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pixie-c06",
      "source_id": "pixie-s1",
      "quote": "In west Penwith, the area of late survival of the Cornish language, spriggans are distinguished from pixies by their malevolent nature",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Penwith barat (west Penwith), spriggan dibedakan dari pixie karena jahat.",
        "en": "In west Penwith spriggans are distinguished from pixies by their malevolence."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pixie-c07",
      "source_id": "pixie-s2",
      "quote": "Pixies sind kleine Fabelwesen aus der englischen Folklore, deren Charakteristika denen von Kobolden und Feen ähneln. Geschichten über diese Wesen sind im Südwesten Englands, in den Regionen Devon und Cornwall, verbreitet.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pixies makhluk dongeng kecil (kleine Fabelwesen) cerita rakyat Inggris (englischen Folklore) mirip kobold (Kobolden) dan peri (Feen), di Devon dan Cornwall.",
        "en": "Pixies are small fabulous beings (kleine Fabelwesen) of English folklore (englischen Folklore) like kobolds (Kobolden) and fairies (Feen), in Devon and Cornwall."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pixie-c08",
      "source_id": "pixie-s2",
      "quote": "So sollen die pixies Wesen sein, die nicht gut genug für das Paradies und nicht schlecht genug für die Hölle waren und daher für ewig auf der Erde bleiben mussten. Nach einer anderen Geschichte sind die pixies Druiden, die nicht zum Christentum konvertierten und zur Strafe von Gott so lange geschrumpft wurden, bis sie sich zum Christentum bekannten.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pixies makhluk yang tak cukup baik untuk surga (Paradies) dan tak cukup jahat untuk neraka (Hölle), atau druid (Druiden) yang tak masuk Kristen (Christentum). Dikecilkan oleh Tuhan (Gott) sebagai hukuman.",
        "en": "Pixies were beings not good enough for paradise (Paradies) nor bad enough for hell (Hölle), or druids (Druiden) who did not convert to Christianity (Christentum)."
      }

      "quote": "The origin of the word pixie is uncertain. It could have come from the Swedish dialectal pyske, meaning 'small fairy'.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Wight adalah kata Jermanik untuk makhluk atau benda; dalam bahasa Inggris Pertengahan kemudian berkembang menjadi sebutan bagi makhluk gaib, dan dalam fantasi modern sering berarti mayat hidup.",
    "en": "A wight is a Germanic word for a being or thing; in later English it came to mean supernatural beings, and in modern fantasy it often means an undead.",
    "claim_ids": [

  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Wight berarti makhluk atau benda; makna umum ini dimiliki kata-kata serumpunnya dalam bahasa Jermanik (Germanic) lain, tetapi pemakaiannya sangat beragam menurut zaman dan daerah. Dalam bahasa Inggris Kuno kata itu dapat merujuk pada apa saja yang ada, lalu menjadi lebih khusus dalam bahasa Inggris Pertengahan, dan dalam fantasi modern wight sering secara khusus berarti mayat hidup. Wight berasal dari wiht (Old English), sekerabat dengan Wicht dalam bahasa Jerman dan vættr dalam bahasa Nordik Kuno, leluhur vätte Swedia dan vætte Denmark.",
      "en": "A wight is a being or thing; this general meaning is shared by its cognates in other Germanic languages, though their use varies greatly by time and place. In Old English it could refer to anything that exists, became more specific in Middle English, and in modern fantasy wights are often specifically undead. The word comes from Old English wiht, cognate with German Wicht and Old Norse vættr, the ancestor of Swedish vätte and Danish vætte.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wight-c01",
        "wight-c02",
        "wight-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam Old English, wiht dipakai untuk makhluk seperti kurcaci dalam mantra XCIIIB serta eoten Grendel dan naga dalam Beowulf, dan sejak Lindisfarne Gospels abad ke-8 mulai bermakna makhluk gaib atau tak duniawi. Menurut artikel de, Wichtel, bentuk kecil dari Wicht, adalah makhluk dalam dongeng dan legenda yang mirip manusia tetapi jauh lebih kecil, hidup berkelompok di bawah tanah, di gua, atau di sudut rumah, umumnya ramah dan diam-diam membantu pekerjaan sehari-hari, dan kadang menukar bayi manusia dengan bayi mereka.",
      "en": "Old English writers applied wiht to the dwarf in the XCIIIB charm and, in Beowulf, to both Grendel and the dragon, and from the 8th-century Lindisfarne Gospels it began to mean supernatural or unearthly beings. According to the de article, the Wichtel, a diminutive of Wicht, is a being of tales and legends that looks human but is much smaller, lives in groups underground, in caves or in hidden corners of houses, is usually friendly and helps unasked with daily work, and sometimes swaps a human baby for its own.",

      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah ini dipakai untuk kurcaci dalam mantra XCIIIB serta Grendel dan naga dalam Beowulf.",
        "en": "The term is used for the dwarf of the XCIIIB charm and the eoten Grendel and the dragon in Beowulf."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wight-c05",
      "source_id": "wight-s1",
      "quote": "The word began to acquire the sense of supernatural or unearthly beings, included in the 8th century Lindisfarne Gospels.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata ini mulai bermakna makhluk gaib atau tak duniawi, seperti dalam Lindisfarne Gospels abad ke-8.",
        "en": "The word began to mean supernatural or unearthly beings, as in the 8th-century Lindisfarne Gospels."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wight-c06",
      "source_id": "wight-s2",
      "quote": "Als Wichtel werden in Märchen, Sagen und Erzählungen Wesen bezeichnet, die von der Gestalt und Art her menschenähnlich sind, aber deutlich kleiner und in eigenen Gemeinschaften lebend. Sie tauchen meist in Gruppen auf, leben unterirdisch, in Höhlen oder in versteckten Ecken in den Häusern der Menschen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wichtel dalam dongeng (Märchen) dan legenda (Sagen) mirip manusia (menschenähnlich) tetapi kecil, hidup berkelompok di bawah tanah (unterirdisch), gua (Höhlen), atau sudut rumah (Häusern).",
        "en": "Wichtel in tales (Märchen) and legends (Sagen) look human (menschenähnlich) but small, living in groups underground (unterirdisch), in caves (Höhlen) or corners of houses (Häusern)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wight-c07",
      "source_id": "wight-s2",
      "quote": "Wichtel oder Wichtelmännchen ist ein Diminutiv von Wicht, einem Begriff, der im Altdeutschen allgemein für lebendes Wesen, Geschöpf stand.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wichtel adalah bentuk kecil (Diminutiv) dari Wicht, yang dalam bahasa Jerman lama (Altdeutschen) berarti makhluk hidup (lebendes Wesen, Geschöpf).",
        "en": "Wichtel is a diminutive (Diminutiv) of Wicht, which in old German (Altdeutschen) meant a living being (lebendes Wesen, Geschöpf)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wight-c08",
      "source_id": "wight-s2",
      "quote": "Im Allgemeinen sind sie den Menschen gegenüber freundlich und helfen ihnen, meist unaufgefordert, bei der täglichen Arbeit. Bisweilen bitten sie ihrerseits die Menschen um Hilfe, manchmal tauschen sie ein menschliches Kind gegen ein eigenes aus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wichtel ramah (freundlich), membantu pekerjaan sehari-hari (täglichen Arbeit), dan kadang menukar anak manusia (menschliches Kind).",
        "en": "Wichtel are friendly (freundlich), help with daily work (täglichen Arbeit), and sometimes swap a human child (menschliches Kind)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wight-c09",
      "source_id": "wight-s1",
      "quote": "In modern fantasy, wights are often specifically undead.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam fantasi modern (modern fantasy), wight sering berarti mayat hidup (undead).",
        "en": "In modern fantasy, wights are often specifically undead."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "identity",
      "searched": "Wight adalah istilah umum bahasa Jermanik untuk \"makhluk\", bukan satu makhluk tertentu; entri ini meringkas pemakaiannya dalam sumber dan padanan Jermannya, Wichtel."
    },
    {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kurcaci Natal (Christmas elf) adalah elf kecil yang dalam budaya berbahasa Inggris tinggal bersama Sinterklas di Kutub Utara dan membantunya membuat mainan.",
    "en": "Christmas elves are diminutive elves that, in English-speaking cultures, live with Santa Claus at the North Pole and help him make toys.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "christmas-elf-c01",
      "christmas-elf-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam budaya berbahasa Inggris (English-speaking), Christmas elves adalah elf kecil yang tinggal bersama Santa Claus (Sinterklas) di Kutub Utara (North Pole) dan menjadi pembantunya. Mereka biasanya digambarkan berpakaian hijau atau merah, bertelinga besar dan runcing, dan bertopi runcing; kebanyakan berwujud humanoid, kadang mamalia berbulu dan berekor. Mereka sering dikatakan membuat mainan di bengkel Santa dan merawat rusa kutubnya.",
      "en": "Across the English-speaking world, Christmas elves are tiny elves who serve as Santa Claus's assistants in his North Pole home. They are usually shown in green or red with large pointed ears and pointed hats; mostly humanoid, sometimes furry, tailed mammals. They are often said to make toys in Santa's workshop and to tend his reindeer.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "christmas-elf-c01",
        "christmas-elf-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kaitan hadiah Natal (Christmas) dengan elf berakar pada nisse atau tomte Skandinavia pada paruh pertama abad ke-19, dan Saint Nicholas sendiri disebut elf dalam A Visit from St. Nicholas (1823): \"a right jolly old elf\". Gambaran elf di bengkel dipopulerkan Godey's Lady's Book lewat sampul edisi Natal 1873 yang menampilkan Santa dikelilingi mainan dan elf. Di Swedia, pada 1891 sang santo telah menyatu dengan Tomten, penjaga pertanian mirip elf atau kurcaci, dan lewat karya Jenny Nyström tokoh itu dikenal sebagai Jultomten. Menurut artikel id, kurcaci Natal dikenal dalam budaya Amerika, Kanada, Irlandia, dan Inggris.",
      "en": "The link between Christmas presents and elves has precedents in the Scandinavian nisse or tomte in the first half of the 19th century, while the 1823 poem A Visit from St. Nicholas even calls the saint \"a right jolly old elf\". The image of elves in a workshop was popularized by Godey's Lady's Book, whose 1873 Christmas cover showed Santa surrounded by toys and elves. In Sweden, by 1891 the saint had merged with Tomten, an elfish or dwarfish farm guardian, and through Jenny Nyström's work the figure became Jultomten.   "short_description": {
    "id": "Gremlin adalah makhluk usil rekaan yang diciptakan pada awal abad ke-20 untuk menjelaskan kerusakan pesawat terbang dan mesin lain, populer di kalangan penerbang Perang Dunia II.",
    "en": "A gremlin is a mischievous fictional creature invented in the early 20th century to explain malfunctions in aircraft and other machinery, popular among World War II airmen.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "gremlin-c01",
      "gremlin-c02",
      "gremlin-c08"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Gremlin adalah makhluk usil rekaan yang diciptakan pada awal abad ke-20, mula-mula untuk menjelaskan kerusakan pesawat terbang, lalu mesin, proses, dan operatornya. Gambarannya sangat beragam. Pemakaian kata ini untuk makhluk yang menyabotase pesawat pertama kali muncul dalam slang Royal Air Force (RAF) di kalangan pilot Britania yang ditempatkan di Malta, Timur Tengah, dan India pada 1920-an, dengan catatan cetak tertua berupa puisi dalam jurnal Aeroplane in Malta pada 10 April 1929.",
      "en": "A gremlin is a mischievous fictional creature invented early in the 20th century, at first to account for aircraft malfunctions and later for machines, processes and their operators. Depictions vary widely. The word for a creature that sabotages aircraft first appeared in Royal Air Force (RAF) slang among British pilots in Malta, the Middle East and India in the 1920s, the earliest printed record being a poem in the journal Aeroplane in Malta on 10 April 1929.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "gremlin-c01",
        "gremlin-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Asal namanya diperdebatkan: ada yang menurunkannya dari kata Inggris Kuno gremian (\"menjengkelkan\"), dan ada pula yang menganggapnya gabungan kata goblin dengan Fremlin, merek bir yang paling banyak tersedia di RAF pada 1920-an. Pada Perang Dunia II (World War II), awak pesawat RAF menyalahkan gremlin atas kecelakaan yang tak dapat dijelaskan; pesawat musuh ternyata mengalami masalah serupa, sehingga gremlin dianggap pengganggu yang tidak memihak. Menurut artikel de, penulis Roald Dahl, yang pernah bertugas di RAF, menerbitkan The Gremlins pada 1943, dan gremlin seperti banyak jenis kobold lain gemar berbuat usil, terutama di bidang mesin dan teknik.",
      "en": "The name's origin is disputed: some derive it from the Old English gremian (\"to vex\"), others see a blend of goblin with Fremlin, the beer most widely available in the RAF in the 1920s. In World War II RAF crews blamed gremlins for otherwise inexplicable accidents; enemy aircraft turned out to have the same troubles, so gremlins were seen as even-handed tricksters. According to the de article, the writer Roald Dahl, who had served in the RAF, published The Gremlins in 1943, and like many kinds of kobold the gremlin loves mischief, above all with machines and technology.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "gremlin-c03",
        "gremlin-c04",
        "gremlin-c05",
        "gremlin-c06",
        "gremlin-c07",
        "gremlin-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "tradition_vs_modern": null,

    {
      "id": "gremlin-s1",
      "url": "https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gremlin",
      "title": "Gremlin",
      "author": null,

    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-s2",
      "url": "https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gremlin",
      "title": "Gremlin",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (de)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "de",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c01",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s1",
      "quote": "A gremlin is a mischievous fictional creature invented at the beginning of the 20th century originally to explain malfunctions in aircraft, and later in other machinery, processes and their operators. Depictions of these creatures vary widely.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gremlin adalah makhluk usil rekaan dari awal abad ke-20 untuk menjelaskan kerusakan pesawat, lalu mesin lain; gambarannya beragam.",
        "en": "A gremlin is a mischievous fictional creature invented in the early 20th century to explain aircraft and later machine malfunctions; depictions vary."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c02",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s1",
      "quote": "Use of the term in the sense of a mischievous creature that sabotages aircraft first arose in Royal Air Force (RAF) slang among British pilots stationed in Malta, the Middle East and India in the 1920s, with the earliest printed record in a poem published in the journal Aeroplane in Malta on 10 April 1929.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata gremlin untuk penyabotase pesawat muncul dalam slang Royal Air Force (RAF) di kalangan pilot Britania (British) di Malta, Middle East (Timur Tengah), dan India pada 1920-an; catatan tertua puisi di Aeroplane in Malta, 10 April 1929.",
        "en": "The term for an aircraft saboteur arose in Royal Air Force (RAF) slang among British pilots in Malta, the Middle East and India in the 1920s; earliest print a poem in Aeroplane in Malta, 10 April 1929."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c03",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s1",
      "quote": "the folklorist John W. Hazen states that some people derive the name from the Old English word gremian, \"to vex\", while Carol Rose, in her book Spirits, Fairies, Leprechauns, and Goblins: An Encyclopedia, attributes the name to a portmanteau of Grimm's Fairy Tales and Fremlin Beer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Folklorist John W. Hazen: sebagian menurunkan namanya dari gremian (Old English, Inggris Kuno) \"menjengkelkan\"; Carol Rose mengaitkannya dengan Grimm's Fairy Tales dan bir Fremlin.",
        "en": "Folklorist John W. Hazen: some derive the name from Old English gremian, to vex; Carol Rose links it to Grimm's Fairy Tales and Fremlin beer."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c04",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s1",
      "quote": "The flight crews blamed gremlins for otherwise inexplicable accidents which sometimes occurred during their flights. Gremlins were also thought at one point to have enemy sympathies, but investigations revealed that enemy aircraft had similar and equally inexplicable mechanical problems.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Awak pesawat menyalahkan gremlin atas kecelakaan tak terjelaskan; pesawat musuh ternyata mengalami masalah serupa.",
        "en": "Flight crews blamed gremlins for inexplicable accidents; enemy aircraft turned out to have the same problems."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c05",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s1",
      "quote": "According to Paul Quinion, it is plausible that the term is a blend of the word \"goblin\" with the name of the manufacturer of the most common beer available in the RAF in the 1920s, Fremlin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Paul Quinion, kata ini mungkin gabungan goblin dengan Fremlin, bir paling umum di RAF pada 1920-an.",
        "en": "According to Paul Quinion, the term may blend goblin with Fremlin, the commonest beer in the RAF in the 1920s."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c06",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s2",
      "quote": "Der britische Schriftsteller Roald Dahl, der auch in der Royal Air Force gedient hatte, veröffentlichte 1943 „The Gremlins“.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penulis Britania (britische Schriftsteller) Roald Dahl, mantan anggota Royal Air Force, menerbitkan The Gremlins pada 1943.",
        "en": "The British writer (britische Schriftsteller) Roald Dahl, who served in the Royal Air Force, published The Gremlins in 1943."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c07",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s2",
      "quote": "Der Gremlin hat, wie viele andere Koboldarten auch, Spaß am Schabernack, vor allem im maschinellen und technischen Bereich.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gremlin, seperti jenis kobold (Koboldarten) lain, gemar berbuat usil (Schabernack), terutama di bidang mesin (maschinellen) dan teknik (technischen).",
        "en": "The Gremlin, like other kinds of kobold (Koboldarten), loves mischief (Schabernack), above all with machines (maschinellen) and technology (technischen)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gremlin-c08",
      "source_id": "gremlin-s1",
      "quote": "This concept of gremlins was popularized during World War II among airmen of the Royal Air Force (RAF) units",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konsep gremlin dipopulerkan pada Perang Dunia II (World War II) di kalangan penerbang Royal Air Force.",
        "en": "The gremlin concept was popularized in World War II among Royal Air Force airmen."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lir atau Ler (\"laut\" dalam bahasa Irlandia Kuno) adalah dewa laut mitologi Irlandia, personifikasi laut, ayah Manannán mac Lir, dan raja dalam kisah The Children of Lir.",
    "en": "Lir or Ler ('sea' in Old Irish) is a sea god of Irish mythology, a personification of the sea, father of Manannán mac Lir and king in the tale The Children of Lir.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lir-c01",
      "lir-c02",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lir atau Ler (\"laut\" dalam Old Irish; Ler bentuk nominatif dan Lir bentuk genitif) adalah dewa laut dalam mitologi Irlandia (Irish mythology). Namanya menunjukkan bahwa ia lebih merupakan personifikasi laut daripada dewa yang berdiri sendiri. Dalam silsilah awal ia bernama Allód, dan ia sepadan dengan Llŷr dalam mitologi Wales. Lir terutama tokoh leluhur, ayah dewa Manannán mac Lir yang sering muncul dalam sastra Irlandia abad pertengahan; dalam mitos Gaelik putranya tampaknya mengambil alih kedudukannya.",
      "en": "Lir or Ler ('sea' in Old Irish; Ler is the nominative and Lir the genitive) is a sea god of Irish mythology. His name suggests he is a personification of the sea rather than a separate deity. In early genealogies he is called Allód, and he matches Llŷr of Welsh mythology. Lir is chiefly an ancestor figure, father of the god Manannán mac Lir who appears often in medieval Irish literature; in Gaelic myth his son seems to take over his position.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lir-c01",
        "lir-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Lir adalah tokoh utama kisah The Children of Lir, walau tidak pasti apakah ia sama dengan ayah Manannán. Dalam kisah itu Lir menjadi saingan Bodb Dearg memperebutkan takhta Tuatha Dé Danann; untuk menenangkannya, Bodb menikahkannya dengan putrinya Aeb, yang melahirkan Fionnuala, Aed, serta si kembar Fiachra dan Conn. Menurut artikel de, setelah Aobh meninggal Lir menikahi saudarinya Aoife, yang karena cemburu mengubah anak-anak itu menjadi angsa; mereka harus mengembara 900 tahun sebelum kembali berwujud manusia.",
      "en": "Lir is the central figure of The Children of Lir, though it is unclear whether he is the same as Manannán's father. In that tale Lir is Bodb Dearg's rival for the kingship of the Tuatha Dé Danann; to appease him Bodb gives him his daughter Aeb, who bears Fionnuala, Aed and the twins Fiachra and Conn. According to the de article, after Aobh dies Lir marries her sister Aoife, who out of jealousy turns the children into swans; they must wander for 900 years before regaining human shape.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lir-c04",
        "lir-c05",
        "lir-c06"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Nibelung (Old Norse: Niflungr) adalah nama pribadi atau nama klan dengan berbagai makna dalam legenda kepahlawanan Jermanik, dan juga bangsa kurcaci pemilik harta Nibelung.",
    "en": "Nibelung (Old Norse Niflungr) is a personal or clan name with several meanings in Germanic heroic legend, and also a race of dwarfs owning the Nibelung hoard.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nibelung-c01",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Istilah Nibelung (Jerman) atau Niflungr (Old Norse) adalah nama pribadi atau nama klan dengan beberapa pemakaian yang saling bersaing dan bertentangan dalam legenda kepahlawanan Jermanik (Germanic). Asal katanya tidak jelas, tetapi sering dikaitkan dengan akar kata Jerman Nebel (\"kabut\"). Istilah ini memberi nama pada epos Middle High German Nibelungenlied. Pemakaian yang paling luas merujuk keluarga kerajaan Burgundia, juga disebut Gibichungen atau Gjúkingar: saudara-saudara raja yang dipimpin Gunther atau Gunnar, yang bertanggung jawab atas kematian Siegfried atau Sigurd dan kemudian dihancurkan di istana Attila.",
      "en": "Germanic heroic legend uses Nibelung (German), or Niflungr in Old Norse, as a personal or family name in several rival and even contradictory senses. Its etymology is unclear, though often tied to German Nebel ('mist'). It gives its name to the Middle High German epic Nibelungenlied. The most widespread use names the Burgundian royal house, also called Gibichungen or Gjúkingar: royal brothers led by Gunther or Gunnar, who bring about the hero Siegfried's (Sigurd's) death and are themselves wiped out later at Attila's court.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nibelung-c01",
        "nibelung-c02",
        "nibelung-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam bahasa Jerman abad pertengahan, Nibelung juga merujuk raja dan penghuni negeri mitos yang didiami kurcaci dan raksasa dalam paruh pertama Nibelungenlied, yang memberi nama \"harta Nibelung\". Menurut artikel es, nibelungo adalah bangsa mitos legenda Jermanik yang dipimpin pangeran Nibelung: kurcaci gelap yang tinggal di dalam bumi, menambang logam mulia terutama emas, dan memiliki harta besar di dasar sungai Rin; Sigfrido (Siegfried) membunuh pangeran Nibelung dan Schilbung setelah bertengkar soal pembagian harta. Richard Wagner kemudian menggubah siklus empat opera El anillo del nibelungo berdasarkan legenda ini.",
      "en": "In medieval German, Nibelung also names the king and people of a mythical land of dwarfs and giants in the first half of the Nibelungenlied, which gives its name to the 'hoard of the Nibelungs'. According to the es article, the Nibelungs are a mythical people of Germanic legend ruled by prince Nibelung: dark dwarfs living deep in the earth, mining precious metals, above all gold, with a huge treasure at the bottom of the Rin (Rhine); Sigfrido (Siegfried) killed the princes Nibelung and Schilbung after quarrelling over how to share the hoard. Richard Wagner later wrote a cycle of four operas, El anillo del nibelungo, based on these legends.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nibelung-c03",
        "nibelung-c05",
        "nibelung-c06",
        "nibelung-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Borvo (juga Bormo) adalah dewa Kelt kuno untuk mata air panas yang menyembuhkan, dipuja di Galia Romawi dan disamakan dengan Apollo.",
    "en": "Borvo (also Bormo) is an ancient Celtic god of hot healing springs, worshipped in Roman Gaul and equated with Apollo.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "borvo-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Borvo, juga Bormo, adalah dewa Kelt (Celtic) kuno untuk mata air panas dan mata air penyembuh. Pemujaannya tercatat di wilayah luas Galia Romawi dan mencapai barat laut Semenanjung Iberia. Dalam interpretatio romana ia biasanya disamakan dengan Apollo, dan di Aix-en-Provence dengan Hercules; pasangan ilahinya yang paling sering adalah dewi Damona. Nama Galia Boruō berarti \"mata air panas\", dari akar kata Proto-Kelt berw- (\"mendidih, menyeduh\").",
      "en": "Borvo, or Bormo, was the old Celtic god who presided over hot, health-giving springs. His cult is recorded across much of Roman Gaul and reached the north-west of the Iberian Peninsula. Under the interpretatio romana he was usually equated with Apollo, and at Aix-en-Provence with Hercules; his most frequent divine partner was the goddess Damona. The Gaulish name Boruō means 'hot spring', from the Proto-Celtic root berw- ('boil, brew').",
      "claim_ids": [
        "borvo-c01",
        "borvo-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Borvo umumnya ditafsirkan sebagai dewa air hangat yang memancar dari mata air panas, yang dianggap berkhasiat; bukti pemujaannya hampir seluruhnya berupa prasasti persembahan di tempat pemandian Galia-Romawi. Sebuah relief dari Entrains menggambarkannya dengan piala, kantong uang, dan piring buah, dan relief dari Vichy menampilkannya duduk telanjang di atas batu memegang cawan berbuih. Banyak nama tempat di dekat mata air berasal dari namanya, seperti Bourbon, Bourbonne, dan La Bourboule. Menurut artikel de, namanya berarti \"yang mendidih/memancar\", dan Borvo masih tersimpan dalam nama Bourbonne-les-Bains dan Bourbon-Lancy.",
      "en": "Borvo is generally read as the god of warm, welling waters of thermal springs held to be curative; the evidence is almost entirely votive inscriptions at Gallo-Roman spas. On a relief from Entrains he holds a cup, a purse and a dish of fruit, and one from Vichy shows him seated naked on a rock holding a bubbling cup. Many place names near springs come from his name, such as Bourbon, Bourbonne and La Bourboule. According to the de article his name means 'the boiling/bubbling one', and Borvo survives in the names Bourbonne-les-Bains and Bourbon-Lancy.",

        "en": "Borvo/Bormo survives in the French place names (französischen Ortsnamen) Bourbonne-les-Bains and Bourbon-Lancy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "borvo-c08",
      "source_id": "borvo-s2",
      "quote": "Der Name Borvo wird vom keltischen Wort für „kochen“ hergeleitet, er hängt mit seiner Funktion als Lokalgottheit bei heißen Quellen zusammen („der Kochende/Sprudelnde“).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Borvo dari kata Kelt untuk mendidih (kochen), terkait perannya sebagai dewa lokal mata air panas (heißen Quellen).",
        "en": "The name Borvo derives from the Celtic word for boil (kochen), linked to his role as local god of hot springs (heißen Quellen)."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Nuckelavee adalah iblis serupa kuda dari cerita rakyat Orkney yang menggabungkan unsur kuda dan manusia, disebut iblis paling keji di Northern Isles Skotlandia.",
    "en": "The nuckelavee is a horse-like demon of Orcadian folklore combining equine and human elements, called the nastiest demon of Scotland's Northern Isles.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nuckelavee-c01",
      "nuckelavee-c02",

  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Nuckelavee atau nuckalavee adalah iblis serupa kuda dari cerita rakyat Orkney (Orcadian folklore) yang menggabungkan unsur kuda dan manusia. Bila dilihat sekilas atau dalam gelap malam ia tampak seperti penunggang kuda biasa, tetapi diamati lebih dekat ia menyerupai kuda tanpa kulit bermata satu dengan sirip di kakinya, dan dari punggungnya tumbuh kepala, tubuh, dan lengan manusia tanpa kulit yang lebih panjang dari biasanya. Folklorist Katharine Briggs menyebutnya \"yang paling keji\" di antara semua iblis Northern Isles Skotlandia.",
      "en": "The nuckelavee or nuckalavee is a horse-like demon of Orcadian folklore that blends horse and human. At a glance, or in the shadows of night, it seems an ordinary horseman, but looked at closely it is a skinless horse with one eye and fins on its legs, from whose back grow a skinless human head, torso and over-long arms. Katharine Briggs judged it the worst (\"the nastiest\") demon in all the Northern Isles of Scotland.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nuckelavee-c01",
        "nuckelavee-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Napasnya dipercaya melayukan tanaman dan membuat ternak sakit, dan ia disalahkan atas kekeringan dan wabah di darat meski terutama hidup di laut. Seperti banyak monster laut lain, ia tak tahan air tawar, sehingga orang yang dikejarnya cukup menyeberangi sungai atau kali. Pada musim panas ia dikurung oleh Mither o' the Sea, roh Orkney kuno yang satu-satunya mampu mengendalikannya. Namanya berasal dari kata Orkney knoggelvi, yang menurut Walter Traill Dennison berarti \"Iblis Laut\"; di Shetland iblis yang sama disebut mukkelevi.",
      "en": "Its breath was thought to wither crops and sicken livestock, and it was blamed for droughts and epidemics on land although it lives mainly in the sea. Like many sea monsters it cannot bear fresh water, so anyone it pursues need only cross a river or stream. Only the Mither o' the Sea, an old Orcadian spirit, can master it, and she keeps it locked away through the summer. Its name comes from Orcadian knoggelvi, which Walter Traill Dennison said means 'Devil of the Sea'; in Shetland the same demon is called mukkelevi.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nuckelavee-c03",
        "nuckelavee-c04",
        "nuckelavee-c05",
        "nuckelavee-c06"

    },
    {
      "id": "Cerita rakyat Orkney sangat dipengaruhi Skandinavia, dan nuckelavee mungkin gabungan kuda air mitologi Kelt dengan makhluk yang dibawa bangsa Nordik. Seperti kelpie, ia mungkin menjadi penjelasan atas kejadian yang tak dapat dipahami penduduk pulau pada masa lalu.",
      "en": "Orcadian folklore owes much to Scandinavian influence, and the nuckelavee may be a blend of a Celtic water horse with a creature brought by the Norsemen. Like the kelpie, it may have explained events the islanders of old could not otherwise understand.",
      "claim_ids": [

      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Barghest adalah anjing hitam mengerikan bergigi dan bercakar besar dalam cerita rakyat Inggris Utara, yang sering dianggap pertanda kematian.",
    "en": "The Barghest is a monstrous black dog with large teeth and claws in Northern English folklore, often held to be an omen of death.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "barghest-c01",
      "barghest-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Inggris Utara (Northern English folklore), Barghest atau Barguest adalah anjing hitam mitos yang mengerikan dengan gigi dan cakar besar; dalam kasus lain nama ini dapat merujuk pada hantu atau elf rumah, terutama di Northumberland dan Durham, seperti Cauld Lad of Hylton. Asal namanya diperdebatkan: mungkin dari burh-ghest (\"hantu kota\"), karena ghost di Inggris Utara diucapkan guest, atau dari kata Jerman Berg-geist (\"hantu gunung\"), Bär-geist (\"hantu beruang\"), atau Bahr-Geist (\"roh usungan jenazah\").",
      "en": "In Northern English folklore the Barghest or Barguest is a monstrous mythical black dog with large teeth and claws; elsewhere, notably in Durham and Northumberland, the name can also mean a household elf or ghost, the Cauld Lad of Hylton being one. The origin of the name is debated: perhaps burh-ghest ('town-ghost'), since 'ghost' was pronounced 'guest' in the north, or German Berg-geist ('mountain ghost'), Bär-geist ('bear-ghost') or Bahr-Geist ('spirit of the funeral bier').",
      "claim_ids": [
        "barghest-c01",
        "barghest-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Yorkshire, salah satu Barghest yang terkenal konon menghantui ngarai terpencil Troller's Gill di Yorkshire Dales; balada \"The Legend of the Troller's Gill\" menceritakan seorang lelaki yang memanggilnya dalam ritual sihir dan kemudian ditemukan tewas dengan bekas luka tak manusiawi di dadanya. Di Durham pada 1870-an, Barghest yang berubah wujud konon tinggal dekat Darlington dan menjelma lelaki tanpa kepala, perempuan tanpa kepala, kucing putih, kelinci, atau anjing hitam. Barghest sering dianggap pertanda kematian: ketika seorang tokoh meninggal, ia muncul diikuti semua anjing setempat dalam semacam iring-iringan jenazah. Ia dapat menjadi tak kasatmata dan berjalan dengan bunyi rantai bergemerincing, dan seperti vampir ia tak dapat menyeberangi sungai.",
      "en": "In Yorkshire a notable Barghest is said to haunt the remote gorge of Troller's Gill in the Yorkshire Dales; the ballad 'The Legend of the Troller's Gill' tells of a man who summoned it in a magic rite and was soon found dead with inhuman marks on his breast. In 1870s Durham a shapeshifting Barghest was said to live near Darlington, taking the form of a headless man, a headless lady, a white cat, a rabbit or a black dog. The Barghest is often an omen of death: when a notable person dies it appears followed by all the local dogs in a kind of funeral procession. It can turn invisible and walk with the sound of rattling chains, and like the vampire it cannot cross rivers.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "barghest-c03",
        "barghest-c04",
        "barghest-c05",
        "barghest-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel de, Barghest (juga Bargtjest, Bo-guest, atau Bargest) adalah anjing hitam mitos yang dipercaya di Inggris Utara, terutama di Yorkshire, yang kadang memasuki kota York untuk mengintai pelancong yang sendirian di jalan-jalan sempit; menurut sebagian sumber ia bukan hantu, melainkan kobold yang dapat berubah menjadi anjing.",
      "en": "According to the de article, the Barghest (also Bargtjest, Bo-guest or Bargest) is a mythical black dog believed in northern England, especially Yorkshire, that sometimes enters the city of York to lie in wait for lone travellers in its narrow streets; some sources say it is not a ghost but a kobold able to turn into a dog.",
